|\varrho^{1/2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)-\varrho_{0}^{1/2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(t_{0},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim M^{2}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}},

(3.96)

where
\varrho_{0}=t_{0}-u
. Then the estimate on
|\mathring{\underline{L}}{\varphi_{\gamma}}|
in (
3.94
) follows
from (
3.96
) for small
\delta
. This, together with
\mathring{\underline{L}}=\mu\mathring{L}+2T
, yields the estimate
for
|T\varphi_{\gamma}|
in (
3.94
) by
(\star)
.
∎

To improve the estimate on
\varphi_{\gamma}
further, one treats
\eta
first since
T=\frac{\partial}{\partial u}-\eta^{X}X
.

Lemma 3.2
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
, it holds that for
\delta>0
small,

|\eta|=\sqrt{g_{XX}\eta^{X}\eta^{X}}\lesssim M\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}.

(3.97)

Proof.

Note that
\eta
is a vector field on
S_{\mathfrak{t},u}
and one has

\mathring{L}\eta^{X}=[T,\mathring{L}]^{X}=\big(\mathscr{D}_{T}\mathring{L}-\mathscr{D}_{\mathring{L}}T\big)^{X}=\not{d}^{X}\mu+2\mu\zeta^{X}.

(3.98)

Then, it follows from (
2.66
) that

\mathring{L}(\varrho^{-2}|\eta|^{2})=\varrho^{-2}\big\{2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\eta|^{2}+\not{d}_{X}\mu\eta^{X}+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{a}\eta^{X}\big\}.

This, together with (
3.93
), (
3.88
) and
(\star)
, yields

|\mathring{L}(\varrho^{-1}|\eta|)|\lesssim M\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}(\varrho^{-1}|\eta|)+M\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-2}.

(3.99)

Thus (
3.97
) follows immediately by integrating (
3.99
) along integral curves of
\mathring{L}
.
∎

Based on Proposition
3.2
and Lemma
3.2
, we can now improve the estimates
on
\varphi_{\gamma}
and
\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}
,
which are independent of
M
.

Proposition 3.3
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with
\delta>0
suitably small, it holds that

|\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\qquad|\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}.

(3.100)

Proof.

First, substituting
T=\frac{\partial}{\partial u}-\eta^{X}X
into (
3.94
) and using
(\star)
and (
3.97
) lead to

\displaystyle|\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}.

(3.101)

Integrating (
3.101
) from
0
to
u
yields the desired estimate on
\varphi_{\gamma}
in (
3.100
) since
\varphi_{\gamma}
vanishes on
C_{0}
.

Next, note that
\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}
satisfies the equation

\begin{split}\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}=-\frac{1}{2\varrho}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+H_{\gamma}-\mathring{L}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}+2\eta^{A}\not{d}_{A}\varphi_{\gamma}+2\mu\zeta^{A}\not{d}_{A}\varphi_{\gamma}\end{split}

due to (
2.70
) and (
3.81
).
Then
|\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}|\lesssim\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}
follows from (
3.82
), (
2.58
) and (
2.66
). Similarly as for (
3.101
), one can get

|\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}.

(3.102)

Then the estimate for
\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}
in (
3.100
) follows by integrating (
3.102
) from
0
to
u
.
∎

One can now use Propositions
3.2
and
3.3
to measure the errors of the components of the frames.

Lemma 3.3
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with
\delta>0
suitably small, it holds that

|\check{L}^{i}|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\ \ |\check{T}^{i}|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},

(3.103)

|\frac{r}{\varrho}-1|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\ \ |{R}^{k}-{\Omega}^{k}|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}.

(3.104)

Proof.

First, it follows from (
3.79
), (
3.100
), and (
2.63
) that

|\mathring{L}\big(\varrho\check{L}^{i}\big)|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}.

(3.105)

Integrating (
3.105
) along integral curves of
\mathring{L}
yields the desired estimate on
\check{L}^{i}
in (
3.103
),
which implies the estimate on
\check{T}^{i}
in (
3.103
) by

\check{T}^{i}=\varphi_{i}-\check{L}^{i}
.

Next, due to
\displaystyle g_{ij}(\check{T}^{i}-\frac{x^{i}}{\varrho})(\check{T}^{j}-\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho})=1
, then

\frac{r^{2}}{\varrho^{2}}=c-\delta_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\check{T}^{j}+2\delta_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\frac{x^{j}}{\varrho}.

Thus,

\frac{r}{\varrho}-1=\frac{2(\varphi_{0}+\varphi_{i}\omega^{i})+2\check{L}^{i}\varphi_{i}-\delta_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\check{L}^{j}-2\delta_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\omega^{j}}{\sqrt{c+(\delta_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\omega^{j})^{2}-\delta_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\check{T}^{j}}+1-\delta_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\omega^{j}},

(3.106)

which implies the first inequality in (
3.104
) by
(\star)
and (
3.103
), while the second one follows from this, (
3.103
) and (
3.104
).
∎

Note that the rotation vector field
R
behaves just as the scaling operator
r\not{\nabla}
under the assumptions
(\star)

and the estimate (
3.88
) as stated in the following lemma, which is similar to
[
28
, Lemma 12.22]
.

Lemma 3.4
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with
\delta>0
suitably small, it holds that

(i) if
\kappa
is a 1-form on
S_{\mathfrak{t},u}
, then

\displaystyle(\kappa\cdot R)^{2}=\big(1+O(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2})\big)r^{2}|\kappa|^{2},

(3.107)

\displaystyle|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}\kappa|^{2}=\big(1+O(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2})\big)r^{2}|\not{\nabla}\kappa|^{2}+O(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}M^{2})|\kappa|^{2};

(3.108)

(ii) if
\Theta
is a 2-form on
S_{\mathfrak{t},u}
, then

|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{R}\Theta|^{2}=r^{2}|\not{\nabla}\Theta|^{2}\big(1+O(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2})\big)+|\Theta|^{2}O(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}M^{2}).

(3.109)

Note that although the
L^{\infty}
estimates for lower order derivatives of some geometrical quantities (such as (
3.88
), (
3.91
) and (
3.93
)) and the
M
-independent estimates for the first order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
(see (
3.94
) and (
3.100
)) obtained
in the above are not enough to close the assumptions
(\star)
, yet the method proving (
3.100
) indicates that it is possible to improve the estimates on
\varphi_{\gamma}
by making use of the equation (
3.81
). Thus, under the assumptions
(\star)
, one can continue to improve the estimates of
\varphi_{\gamma}
up to the
(N-1)^{th}
order derivatives as given in the following proposition, whose proof is exactly same as those in
[
11
, Section 6]
and thus omitted.

Proposition 3.4
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with
\delta>0
small, it holds that for any operator
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
and
k\leq N-1
,

\begin{split}&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k+1;i,l}\not{d}x^{j}|\lesssim\delta^{-i},\quad|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k;i,l}\check{\chi}|\lesssim M\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2},\quad|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k+1;i,l}R|\lesssim M\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2},\\
&|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k;i,l}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k;i,l}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k;i,l}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|\lesssim M\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\\
&|Z^{k+1;i,l}\check{L}^{j}|\lesssim M\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\quad|Z^{k+1;i,l}\upsilon|\lesssim M\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2},\\
&|Z^{k+1;i,l}\mu|\lesssim M\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}},\quad|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k;i,l}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}|\lesssim M\delta^{-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\end{split}

(3.110)

where
(k;i,l)
means that the number of
Z
is
k
, the number of
T
is
i
, and the number of
\varrho\mathring{L}
is
l
.

Proposition 3.5
.

Under the same assumptions as in Proposition
3.4
, it holds that for
k\leq N-1
,

\displaystyle|Z^{k+2;i+1,l+1}\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},

(3.111)

\displaystyle|Z^{k+1;i,l+1}\varphi_{\gamma}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)|\lesssim\delta^{1-i-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}.

(3.112)

Finally, it is noted that under the assumptions
(\star)
,
we have obtained not only the estimates with
M
dependent bounds in Proposition
3.4
, but also more refined estimates independent of
M

in Proposition
3.5
.
On the other hand, if starting with Proposition
3.5
and repeating
the above analysis, one can improve the conclusions in
3.4

such that all the related constants are independent of
M
when
k\leq N-3
. Therefore,
from now on, we may apply these estimates without the constant
M
since
N
could be chosen large enough.

4
Energy estimates for the linearized equation and some higher order
L^{2}
estimates

To close the bootstrap assumptions
(\star)
, one needs further refined estimates than those derived in Section
3
. To this end, we plan to construct some suitable energies for higher order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
. Note that
\varphi_{\gamma}
satisfies the nonlinear equation (
2.77
), and each
derivative of
\varphi_{\gamma}
also fulfills a similar equation with the same metric. Thus, in the section, we first focus on
the energy estimates for any smooth
function
\Psi
solving the following linear equation

\mu\Box_{g}\Psi=\Phi

(4.113)

for a given function
\Phi
, where
\Psi
and its derivatives vanish on
C_{0}^{\mathfrak{t}}
. The following divergence theorem in
D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}
will be used, whose proof follows that of
[
28
, Lemma 10.12]
.

Lemma 4.1
.

For any vector field
J
, it holds that

\begin{split}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}J^{\alpha}d\nu_{\not{g}}du^{\prime}d\tau=&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\big(-J_{T}-\mu J_{\mathring{L}}\big)d\nu_{\not{g}}du^{\prime}-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\big(-J_{T}-\mu J_{\mathring{L}}\big)d\nu_{\not{g}}du^{\prime}\\
&-\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}J_{\mathring{L}}d\nu_{\not{g}}d\tau.\end{split}

(4.114)

As explained in Section
1.2.1
, the vectorfields
J^{\prime}
s used in
[
11
]
are not suitable for (
1.8
)
due to the slow time decay of the solutions to the 2D wave equations. We will choose
some new vectorfields
J^{\prime}
s in (
4.114
), which are expressed as

\displaystyle J_{1}:=-\varrho^{2m}g^{\alpha\kappa}Q_{\kappa\beta}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\partial_{\alpha},\quad J_{2}:=-g^{\alpha\kappa}Q_{\kappa\beta}\mathring{\underline{L}}^{\beta}\partial_{\alpha},

(4.115)

\displaystyle J_{3}:=\big(\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}\Psi-\frac{1}{4}\Psi^{2}\mathscr{D}^{\alpha}(\varrho^{2m-1})\big)\partial_{\alpha},

(4.116)

where
m\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4})
is a fixed constant and
Q
is the
energy-momentum tensor field
of
\Psi
defined as

\begin{split}Q_{\alpha\beta}&=Q_{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]:=(\partial_{\alpha}\Psi)(\partial_{\beta}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}g_{\alpha\beta}g^{\nu\chi}(\partial_{\nu}\Psi)(\partial_{\chi}\Psi)\\
&=(\partial_{\alpha}\Psi)(\partial_{\beta}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}g_{\alpha\beta}\big\{|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}-\mu^{-1}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)(\mathring{L}\Psi)\big\}.\end{split}

For each
J_{i}
(
1\leq i\leq 3
), the corresponding terms in (
4.114
) will be analyzed respectively.

We start with the estimates of the right hand side of (
4.114
). Note that the components of
Q_{\alpha\beta}
relative to
\{\mathring{L},\mathring{\underline{L}},X\}
are

\begin{split}&Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}=(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2},\quad Q_{\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{\underline{L}}}=(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2},\quad Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}}=\mu|\not{d}\Psi|^{2},\\
&Q_{\mathring{L}X}=(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}_{X}\Psi),\quad Q_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}=(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)(\not{d}_{X}\Psi),\\
&Q_{XX}=\frac{1}{2}(\not{d}_{X}\Psi)(\not{d}_{X}\Psi)+\frac{1}{2}\not{g}_{XX}\mu^{-1}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)(\mathring{L}\Psi).\end{split}

Then it follows from (
4.114
)-(
4.115
) that

\begin{split}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{1}}^{\alpha}=&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\frac{1}{2}\mu\varrho^{2m}\big((\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\big)-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\frac{1}{2}\mu\varrho^{2m}\big((\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\big)\\
&+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\varrho^{2m}(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}\end{split}

(4.117)

and

\begin{split}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{2}}^{\alpha}=&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\frac{1}{2}\big((\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2}+\mu^{2}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\big)-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\frac{1}{2}\big((\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2}+\mu^{2}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\big)\\
&+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\mu|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}.\end{split}

(4.118)

While (
4.114
) and (
4.116
) yield

Lemma 4.2
.

It holds that

\begin{split}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha}=&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mu\mathring{L}\Psi)+\frac{1}{4}\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}((2m-1)(\mu-2)+\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta})\bigg)\\
&-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mu\mathring{L}\Psi)+\frac{1}{4}\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}((2m-1)(\mu-2)+\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta})\bigg)\\
&+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\bigg(-\frac{1}{4}\textrm{tr}\chi\cdot\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}-\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi\mathring{L}\Psi\bigg).\end{split}

(4.119)

Proof.

Applying (
4.114
) to
J_{3}
yields

\begin{split}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha}=&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(T\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mu\mathring{L}\Psi)+\frac{2m-1}{4}\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}(\mu-1)\Big)\\
&-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\Big(-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(T\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mu\mathring{L}\Psi)+\frac{2m-1}{4}\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}(\mu-1)\Big)\\
&+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\big(-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mathring{L}\Psi)+\frac{2m-1}{4}\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}\big).\end{split}

(4.120)

Note that
\Psi
vanishes on
u=0
. Then

\begin{split}\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}&=\int_{0}^{u}\Big(\frac{\partial}{\partial u^{\prime}}\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime}}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}\Big)du^{\prime}\\
&=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\Big(-(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}+2\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi T\psi+\varrho^{2m-1}\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}\Psi^{2}\Big),\end{split}

which implies

\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(T\Psi)=-\frac{1}{4}\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\big(-(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}+\varrho^{2m-1}\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}\Psi^{2}\big).

(4.121)

Taking
t=t_{0}
in (
4.121
) gives

\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(T\Psi)=-\frac{1}{4}\int_{S_{t_{0},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\big(-(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}+\varrho^{2m-1}\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}\Psi^{2}\big).

(4.122)

Thanks to the following identity

\begin{split}\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}&=\int_{S_{t_{0},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\frac{\partial}{\partial\tau}\Big(\int_{S_{\tau,u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}\Big)d\tau\\
&=\int_{S_{t_{0},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}d\nu_{\not{g}}+\int_{C^{\mathfrak{t}}_{u}}\Big(\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2})+\textrm{tr}\chi\cdot\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}\Big)d\nu_{\not{g}}d\tau,\end{split}

then (
4.121
) becomes

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(T\Psi)=&-\frac{1}{4}\int_{S_{t_{0},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\int_{C^{\mathfrak{t}}_{u}}\Big(\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2})+\textrm{tr}\chi\cdot\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi^{2}\Big)\\
&+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\big(-(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}+\varrho^{2m-1}\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}\Psi^{2}\big).\end{split}

(4.123)

Substituting (
4.123
) and (
4.122
) into (
4.120
) yields (
4.119
).
∎

It follows from (
4.119
) and (
4.117
) together with (
3.80
) that

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu(\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{1}}^{\alpha}-\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha})\\
=&\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\Big(\mu\varrho^{2m}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\mu\varrho^{2m}(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\mu\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mathring{L}\Psi)+m\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}+O(\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\Psi^{2})\Big)\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\Big(\mu\varrho^{2m}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\mu\varrho^{2m}(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\mu\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi(\mathring{L}\Psi)+m\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}+O(\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\Psi^{2})\Big)\\
&+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\big((\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+O(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2})\big),\end{split}

(4.124)

here one has used the estimate
|\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}|\lesssim\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}
due to (
2.67
) and the estimates in Section
3
.

In order to estimate
\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\Psi^{2}
and

\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{C^{\mathfrak{t}}_{u}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}
in (
4.124
), one needs
the following inequality.

Lemma 4.3
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
, it holds that for any
f\in C^{1}(D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u})
vanishing on
C_{0}^{t}
,

\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\mu f^{2}+\delta\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\varrho^{-1}f^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\mu f^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(|\mathring{L}(f^{2})|+\delta|T(\varrho^{-1}f^{2})|\big).

(4.125)

Proof.

It follows from Lemma 3.4 in
[
22
]
that for any vectorfield
J
,

\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}J^{\alpha}=-\mathring{L}(\mu J_{\mathring{L}}+J_{T})-T(J_{\mathring{L}})+\not{div}(\mu\not{J})-\mu(\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}+\textrm{tr}\chi)J_{\mathring{L}}-\textrm{tr}\chi J_{T},

(4.126)

where
\not{J}
is the projection of
J
on
S_{{\mathfrak{t}},u}
.
Set
J=f^{2}\mathring{L}
and
J=\delta\varrho^{-1}\mu^{-1}f^{2}T
in (
4.126
) respectively, and then
use (
4.114
) to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\mu f^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\mu f^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big((\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)f^{2}+\mu\mathring{L}(f^{2})\big),

(4.127)

\displaystyle\delta\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\varrho^{-1}f^{2}

\displaystyle=\delta\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\mu\varrho^{-1}\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}f^{2}+T(\varrho^{-1}f^{2})\big).

(4.128)

By adding (
4.127
) and (
4.128
) together with the estimates on the coefficients in the right hand sides
of (
4.127
)-(
4.128
), one arrives at

\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\mu f^{2}+\delta\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\varrho^{-1}f^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\mu f^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\varrho^{-1}f^{2}+\mu|\mathring{L}(f^{2})|+\delta|T(\varrho^{-1}f^{2})|\big).

Hence (
4.125
) follows directly from Gronwall’s inequality.
∎

Let
f=\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}/2}\varrho^{m-3/4}\Psi
in (
4.125
). Using the facts that

|\mathring{L}(f^{2})|\lesssim\varrho^{-1}f^{2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-1/2}|\mathring{L}\Psi|^{2}

and

|T(\varrho^{-1}f^{2})|\lesssim\delta^{-1}\varrho^{-1}f^{2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|T\Psi|^{2},

one then gets

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\mu\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\Psi^{2}+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}\\
\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\mu\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\Psi^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\bigg(\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|T\Psi|^{2}\bigg).\end{split}

(4.129)

Substituting (
4.129
) into (
4.124
) yields

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mu\big(\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{1}}^{\alpha}-\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha}\big)\\
\gtrsim&\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\bigg(\varrho^{2m}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\varrho^{2m}(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}\bigg)-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\bigg(|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\Psi^{2}\bigg)\\
&+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\bigg(\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\delta^{2}\varrho^{2m-5/2}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2}\bigg).\end{split}

(4.130)

By (
4.130
) and the identity (
4.118
), it is natural to define the following energies and fluxes

\displaystyle E_{1}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u):=\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\bigg(\varrho^{2m}(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\varrho^{2m}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}\bigg),

(4.131)

\displaystyle E_{2}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u):=\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}((\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)^{2}+|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}),

(4.132)

\displaystyle F_{1}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u):=\displaystyle\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2},

(4.133)

\displaystyle F_{2}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u):=\displaystyle\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}.

(4.134)

Next, we treat the left hand side of (
4.114
).
Set

V_{1}=\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L},\qquad V_{2}=\mathring{\underline{L}}.

Direct computations give that

\displaystyle\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{1}}^{\alpha}=-\Phi\varrho^{2m}(\mathring{L}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\alpha\beta},

(4.135)

\displaystyle\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{2}}^{\alpha}=-\Phi(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{2})}\pi_{\alpha\beta},

(4.136)

\displaystyle\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha}=\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi\Phi+\Big(-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}\Psi})+\frac{1}{2}\mu\varrho^{2m-1}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{4}\Psi^{2}(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Big((2m-2)\varrho^{-1}(\mu-2)+\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}+(\mu-1)\textrm{tr}\chi\Big).

(4.137)

Recall that the components of
{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{i})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}
in the
frame
\{\mathring{L},\mathring{\underline{L}},X\}
have been given in (
2.59
)-(
2.61
).
To compute
Q^{\alpha\beta}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{i})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}
in (
4.135
)-(
4.136
), one can use the
components of the metric in the frame
\{\mathring{L},\underline{\mathring{L}},X\}
, given in (
2.74
), to derive directly that

\begin{split}&-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
=&-\frac{1}{4}\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}}Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}}-\frac{1}{8}\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{\underline{L}}}Q_{\mathring{L}\mathring{L}}+\frac{1}{2}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{1})}\pi_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}^{X}Q_{\mathring{L}X}-\frac{1}{2}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(V_{1})}\not{\pi}^{XX}Q_{XX}\\
=&\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\mu+\mu(m-\frac{1}{2})\varrho^{2m-1}\bigg)|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\bigg(m(\mu-2)\varrho^{2m-1}-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\mu\bigg)(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\varrho^{2m}\bigg(\not{d}^{X}\mu\\
&+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{a}\bigg)(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}_{X}\Psi)-\frac{1}{2}\mu\varrho^{2m}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\textrm{tr}\chi(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)\end{split}

(4.138)

and

\begin{split}&-\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{2})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
=&\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\mathring{\underline{L}}\mu+\mu\mathring{L}\mu+c^{-1}\mu Tc+c^{-1}\mu^{2}\mathring{L}c+\mu^{2}\textrm{tr}\chi-2c^{-1}\mu^{2}\not{d}_{X}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{a}\bigg)|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\\
&-\bigg(2\mu c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{a}+\not{d}^{X}\mu\bigg)(\not{d}_{X}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi)-\mu\not{d}^{X}\mu(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}_{X}\Psi)\\
&+\bigg(\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}Tc+\frac{1}{2}\mu c^{-1}\mathring{L}c-c^{-1}\mu\not{d}^{X}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2}\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\bigg)(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi).\end{split}

(4.139)

We first treat the terms involving
J_{1}
and
J_{3}
.

Combining (
4.135
) and (
4.137
) and using (
4.138
),
\frac{1}{2}<m<\frac{3}{4}
and
\mu<2
, one can get by the
estimates in Section
3
that

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{1}}^{\alpha}-\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha}\big)\\
=&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\mu(m-1)\varrho^{2m-1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\mu-\frac{1}{2}\mu\varrho^{2m}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}\\
&{+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}\big(m\varrho^{-1}(\mu-2)-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu\big)(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}(\not{d}_{X}\mu+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{a})(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}^{X}\Psi)}\\
&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{4}\Psi^{2}(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Big((2m-2)\varrho^{-1}(\mu-2)+\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}+(\mu-1)\textrm{tr}\chi\Big)\\
&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}\Psi})\\
\leq&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\big(\mathring{L}\mu-\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\mu(\mathring{L}\Psi)^{2}\\
&{+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}(\not{d}_{X}\mu+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{a})(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\not{d}^{X}\Psi)-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(\mathring{L}\Psi)(\mathring{\underline{L}\Psi})}\\
&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{4}(2m-1)\varrho^{2m-2}\Psi^{2}(\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}+(\mu-1)\textrm{tr}\chi)\\
\lesssim&|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}\big)\\
&+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}.\end{split}

(4.140)

The last integral in (
4.140
) can be estimated by setting
f=\varrho^{m-3/4}\Psi
in (
4.125
) as

\begin{split}\delta\int_{C_{u}^{t}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\Psi^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\varrho^{2m-1/2}|\mathring{L}\Psi|^{2}+\delta^{2}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}+{\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}}\big)\\
\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\Psi^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\delta^{2}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}\big)\\
&+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}.\end{split}

This, together with Gronwall’s inequality, yields

\delta\int_{C_{u}^{t}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}\lesssim\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\Psi^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\delta^{2}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}\big).

Thus

\begin{split}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}\Psi^{2}\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\Psi^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\delta^{2}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}\big).\end{split}

(4.141)

Substituting (
4.141
) into (
4.140
) yields

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{1}}^{\alpha}-\mu\mathscr{D}_{\alpha}{J_{3}}^{\alpha}\big)\\
\lesssim&|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-5/2}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|^{2}\big)\\
&+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{-1/2}(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\Psi)^{2}+\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\Psi^{2}.\end{split}

(4.142)

It follows from (
4.130
), (
4.142
) and Gronwall’s inequality that

\begin{split}E_{1}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)+F_{1}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)\lesssim&E_{1}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\Psi^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}E_{2}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|.\end{split}

(4.143)

It remains to treat (
4.136
). Recalling (
4.139
) and estimating each coefficient in it by Proposition
3.4
, one can get

\begin{split}&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\frac{1}{2}\mu Q^{\alpha\beta}[\Psi]{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(V_{2})}\pi_{\alpha\beta}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(|\not{d}\Psi|^{2}+\delta\tau^{-1}|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|\cdot|\not{d}\Psi|+\delta\tau^{-1}|\mathring{L}\Psi|\cdot|\not{d}\Psi|+\tau^{-1/2}|\mathring{L}\Psi|\cdot|\mathring{\underline{L}\Psi|}\big)\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{2}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-m-\frac{1}{2}}E_{2}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-m-\frac{1}{2}}E_{1}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(4.144)

Then, it follows from (
4.118
), (
4.132
), (
4.134
), (
4.136
), (
4.144
) and Gronwall’s inequality that

E_{2}[\Psi](t,u)+F_{2}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)\lesssim E_{2}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|\Phi|\cdot|\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-m-\frac{1}{2}}E_{1}[\Psi](\tau,u)d\tau.

(4.145)

Combining (
4.143
) with (
4.145
) and using Gronwall’s inequality again,
due to
m\in(\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{4})
, one concludes finally that

\begin{split}&\delta E_{2}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta F_{2}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)+E_{1}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)+F_{1}[\Psi](\mathfrak{t},u)\\
\lesssim&\delta E_{2}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+E_{1}[\Psi](t_{0},u)+\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}}\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\Psi^{2}+\delta\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|\\
&+|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|.\end{split}

(4.146)

(
4.146
) will be used to derive the energy estimates for
\varphi_{\gamma}
and its derivatives. To this end, one can choose
\Psi=\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}:=Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}
and then
\Phi=\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}:=\mu\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}
(
k\leq 2N-6
)
in (
4.146
). Note that

\begin{split}\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}&=\mu\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=\mu[\Box_{g},Z]\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+Z\big(\mu\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\big)-(Z\mu)\Box_{g}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&=\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}+(Z+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z)}\chi)\Phi_{\gamma}^{k},\end{split}

(4.147)

where

\begin{split}&{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}=\big({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi^{\nu\beta}-\frac{1}{2}g^{\nu\beta}\textrm{tr}_{g}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi\big)\partial_{\nu}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\partial_{\beta},\\
&{\vphantom{\chi}}{}^{(Z)}\chi=\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}_{g}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi-\mu^{-1}Z\mu,\\
&\Psi_{\gamma}^{0}=\varphi_{\gamma},\quad\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}=\mu\Box_{g}\varphi_{\gamma}\end{split}

(4.148)

with
\textrm{tr}_{g}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi=g^{\alpha\beta}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(Z)}\pi_{\alpha\beta}
and
\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}
being
the right hand side of (
2.77
).
Consequently, for
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=Z_{k+1}Z_{k}\cdots Z_{1}\varphi_{\gamma}
with
Z_{i}\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
, one can derive
by (
4.147
) and an induction argument that

\begin{split}\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}=&\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big)\big(\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}C_{\gamma}^{k-j}\big)\\
&+\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z_{k+1})}C_{\gamma}^{k}+\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{1})}\chi\big)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0},\qquad k\geq 1,\\
\Phi_{\gamma}^{1}=&\big(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{1})}\chi\big)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}+\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z_{1})}C_{\gamma}^{0}.\end{split}

(4.149)

To estimate
{\vphantom{\chi}}{}^{(Z)}\chi
and
\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}

in (
4.149
), by

\textrm{tr}_{g}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi=-2\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+\textrm{tr}_{\not{g}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}

and (
2.59
)-(
2.61
), we have that

\begin{split}&{\vphantom{\chi}}{}^{(T)}\chi=-\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}Tc-\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mu\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-\mu\textrm{tr}_{\not{g}}\chi,\\
&{\vphantom{\chi}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}\chi=\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}+2,\\
&{\vphantom{\chi}}{}^{(R)}\chi=\upsilon\textrm{tr}{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}\upsilon(\mathring{L}c)-c^{-1}\upsilon\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}Rc.\end{split}

(4.150)

In addition, in the null frame
\{\mathring{\underline{L}},\mathring{L},X\}
, the
term
\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}
can be written as

\begin{split}\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}=&-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\big({{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{\underline{L}}}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\big({{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{L}}\big)+\not{\textrm{div}}\big(\mu{\vphantom{\not{C}}}^{(Z)}\not{C}_{\gamma}^{k}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\big(\mathring{L}\mu+\mu\textrm{tr}{\chi}+2\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}\big){{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{L}}-\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{\underline{L}}},\end{split}

(4.151)

where

\begin{split}&{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{L}}={\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}(\not{d}^{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\\
&{{\vphantom{C}}{}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,\mathring{\underline{L}}}=-2({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{LT}+\mu^{-1}{\vphantom{\pi}}{}^{(Z)}\pi_{TT})(\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}(\not{d}^{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}_{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\\
&\mu{{\vphantom{\not{C}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{C}_{\gamma}^{k}}_{,X}=-\frac{1}{2}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}(\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-\frac{1}{2}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}(\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+{\vphantom{{\pi}}}^{(Z)}{\pi}_{\mathring{L}T}(\not{d}_{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+\frac{1}{2}\mu\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}\not{d}_{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}.\end{split}

(4.152)

A direct substitution of (
4.152
) into (
4.151
) would result in a lengthy and complicated equation for
\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}
. To overcome this difficulty and to get the desired estimates efficiently, we follow the ideas in
[
25
]
to decompose
\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}
as

\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{k}={\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{k}+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{k},

(4.153)

where

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{k}=&\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}\big(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\big)-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}(\not{d}^{X}\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}(\not{d}^{X}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})\\
&+({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\tilde{T}T})(\mathring{L}^{2}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k})+\frac{1}{2}\mu\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{{\not{\pi}}}}^{(Z)}{\not{\pi}}\not{\triangle}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}\not{\triangle}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\end{split}

(4.154)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}=&\mathring{L}({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\tilde{T}T})\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}-\big(\frac{1}{2}\not{\nabla}^{X}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}-\frac{1}{4}\mathring{\underline{L}}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\big)\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&-(\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}-\not{d}_{X}{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T})\not{d}^{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}\big(\mu\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}\big)\cdot\not{d}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\nabla}^{X}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X})\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}-\frac{1}{2}(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X})\not{d}^{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k},\qquad\end{split}

(4.155)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{k}=&\Big\{\textrm{tr}{\chi}({\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\mathring{L}T}+{\vphantom{\pi}}^{(Z)}\pi_{\tilde{T}T})+(\frac{1}{4}\mu\textrm{tr}{\chi}+\frac{1}{2}\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta})\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}-\frac{1}{2}\not{d}^{X}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\Big\}\mathring{L}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\Big\{(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi})({\xi}_{X}+\mu\zeta_{X})+(\mu\textrm{tr}\chi-\mathring{L}\mu){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}+\textrm{tr}{\chi}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}X}\Big\}\not{d}^{X}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}.\end{split}

(4.156)

Note that all the terms in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{k}
are the products of the deformation tensor and the second
order derivatives of
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
, except the first term containing the factor of the form
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
(see (
4.154
)). It should be emphasized here that such a structure is crucial in our analysis since
\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
is the derivative of
\varphi_{\gamma}
and by (
3.81
),
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}=H_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
admits the better smallness and the faster time decay than those for
\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
and
\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\chi}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
separately. In addition,
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k}

collects all the products of the first order derivatives of the deformation tensor and the first order derivatives of

\Psi_{\gamma}^{k}
, while
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{k}
denotes all the other terms.

The explicit expression for
\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}
obtained from (
4.149
)-(
4.150
) and (
4.153
)-(
4.156
) will be used to estimate the corresponding last two integrals in (
4.146
). Due to the structure of (
4.146
) for
\Psi=\Psi_{\gamma}^{k+1}
, it is natural to define the corresponding weighted energy and flux as in
[
25
]
:

\displaystyle E_{i,p+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\gamma=0}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=p}\delta^{2l}E_{i}[Z^{\alpha}\varphi_{\gamma}](\mathfrak{t},u),\quad i=1,2,

(4.157)

\displaystyle F_{i,p+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\gamma=0}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha|=p}\delta^{2l}F_{i}[Z^{\alpha}\varphi_{\gamma}](\mathfrak{t},u),\quad i=1,2,

(4.158)

\displaystyle E_{i,\leq p+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{0\leq n\leq p}E_{i,n+1}(\mathfrak{t},u),\quad i=1,2,

(4.159)

\displaystyle F_{i,\leq p+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)

\displaystyle=\sum_{0\leq n\leq p}F_{i,n+1}(\mathfrak{t},u),\quad i=1,2,

(4.160)

where
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{\alpha}
. We will treat these weighted energies in subsequent sections.

Next, we need to carry out the higher order
L^{2}
estimates for some related quantities
so that the last two terms of (
4.146
) can be absorbed by the left hand side, and hence the higher order energy
estimates on (
2.77
) can be done.
To this end, we now state two elementary lemmas, whose analogous results
can be found in
[
25
, Lemma 7.3]
,
[
28
, Lemma 12.57]
and
[
11
, Lemma 8.1, 8.2]
.

Lemma 4.4
.

For any function
\psi\in C^{1}(D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u})
vanishing on
C_{0}
, it holds that for small
\delta>0
,

\displaystyle\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\big(|\mathring{\underline{L}}\psi|^{2}+\mu^{2}|\mathring{L}\psi|^{2}\big),

(4.161)

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\big(|\mathring{\underline{L}}\psi|^{2}+\mu^{2}|\mathring{L}\psi|^{2}\big).

(4.162)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\big(E_{2}[\psi](\mathfrak{t},u)+\varrho^{-2m}E_{1}[\psi](\mathfrak{t},u)\big),

(4.163)

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\psi^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{2}\big(E_{2}[\psi](\mathfrak{t},u)+\varrho^{-2m}E_{1}[\psi](\mathfrak{t},u)\big).

(4.164)

Furthermore,

E_{1,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)\lesssim\delta^{2}\varrho^{2m-2}E_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta^{2}\varrho^{-2}E_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u).

(4.165)

Lemma 4.5
.

Assume that
(\star)
holds for small
\delta>0
. Then for any
f\in C(D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u})
,

F(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta):=\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}f(\tau,u,\vartheta)d\tau
admits the following estimate:

\|F\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\leq(1+C\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}})\sqrt{\varrho({\mathfrak{t}},u)}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\varrho(\tau,u)}}\|f\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau.

(4.166)

Similarly to
[
11
, Section 8]
, one can use Lemma
4.4
and
4.5
to derive
L^{2}
estimates for higher order derivatives of

{\check{\chi}}
,
\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}
,
{\not{\pi}}_{XX}
,
\check{L}
,
\upsilon
and
x^{j}
. The proof are
analogous to those in
[
11
]
except different time decay rates, so will be omitted here.

Proposition 4.1
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with small
\delta>0
, it holds that for
k\leq 2N-6
,

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\mathfrak{t}^{1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(4.167)

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|(\mathfrak{t}^{-1}Z^{k+2}x^{i},\not{\mathcal{L}}^{k+1}_{Z}\not{g})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}+\mathfrak{t}^{1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(4.168)

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi},\mathfrak{t}^{-2}Z^{k+1}\upsilon)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\mathfrak{t}^{-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(4.169)

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|(Z^{k+1}\mu,\mathfrak{t}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}+\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(4.170)

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\big({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi},{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}},{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\big)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+{\mathfrak{t}}^{1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(4.171)

\displaystyle\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(4.172)

where
l
is the number of
T
in the corresponding derivatives and
\tilde{E}_{i,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)=\sup_{t_{0}\leq\tau\leq{\mathfrak{t}}}E_{i,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)

(i=1,2)
.

5
L^{2}
estimates for the highest order derivatives of tr
\chi
and
\not{\triangle}\mu

Analogously to
[
11
, Section 9]
, due to (
4.147
) and (
4.155
), the top orders of derivatives of
\varphi
,
\chi
and
\mu
for the energy estimates in (
4.146
) are
2N-4
,
2N-5
and
2N-4
respectively. However, as shown in Proposition
4.1
, the
L^{2}
estimates for the
(2N-5)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of
\chi
and
(2N-4)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of
\mu
can be controlled by the
(2N-3)
order energy of
\varphi
. So there is a mismatch here.
To overcome this difficulty, we need to deal with
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
and
\not{\triangle}\mu
with the corresponding top order derivatives as in
[
11
]
. Since the time decays of these derivatives are crucial, we will give all the details of analysis for the 2D case here. Furthermore, different from the 4D case in
[
11
]
,
we also need to improve the
L^{2}
estimate of
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}
in (
4.169
)
so that the last term of (
4.146
) can be handled despite the slow time decay rate.

5.1
Estimates for the derivatives of tr
\chi

Due to (
3.84
), tr
\chi
satisfies a transport equation as

\begin{split}\mathring{L}(\textrm{tr}{\chi})=&c^{-1}\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}-c^{-1}(\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{0}+\varphi_{a}\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{a})-c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}-\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}(\mathring{L}c)\textrm{tr}\chi\\
&-c^{-1}\textrm{tr}\chi(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})-|{{\chi}}|^{2}+c^{-2}f(\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\varphi,\not{g})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\varphi\cdot\not{d}\varphi\\
(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})^{2}\\
(\mathring{L}\varphi)(\mathring{L}\varphi)\\
(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})\mathring{L}\varphi\end{array}\right).\end{split}

(5.173)

In addition, (
3.81
) is rewritten as

\begin{split}\mu\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}=\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}-\tilde{H}_{\gamma},\end{split}

(5.174)

where
\tilde{H}_{\gamma}=H_{\gamma}-\mu\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}
.
Then (
5.173
) can be written as

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\big(\textrm{tr}\chi-E)=\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)\textrm{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{\varrho^{2}}-|\check{\chi}|^{2}+e,\end{split}

(5.175)

which contains only the first or zeroth order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
on the right hand side, where

\displaystyle E=

\displaystyle c^{-1}\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-\frac{3}{2}c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta},

(5.176)

\displaystyle e=

\displaystyle c^{-2}f(\not{d}x,\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\varphi,\not{g})\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\not{d}\varphi\cdot\not{d}\varphi\\
(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})^{2}\\
(\mathring{L}\varphi)(\mathring{L}\varphi)\\
(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})\mathring{L}\varphi\end{array}\right).

Set
F^{k}=\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi-\not{d}Z^{k}E
with
Z\in\{\varrho\mathring{L},T,R\}
. It then
follows from (
5.175
) inductively that

\begin{split}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}F^{k}=&\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)F^{k}\\
&+\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)\not{d}Z^{k}E-\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})+e^{k},\end{split}

(5.181)

where for
k\geq 1
,

\begin{split}e^{k}=&\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}e^{0}+\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k-1}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},Z]}F^{k_{2}}\\
&+\sum_{k_{1}\leq k-1}Z^{k-k_{1}}\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)\not{d}Z^{k_{1}}\textrm{tr}\chi\end{split}

(5.182)

and

\begin{split}e^{0}=&\not{d}e+\not{d}\big(-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)\textrm{tr}\chi.\end{split}

(5.183)

Note that for any one-form
\kappa
on
S_{{\mathfrak{t}},u}
, it holds that

\mathring{L}(\varrho^{2}|\kappa|^{2})=-2\varrho^{2}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\kappa|^{2}+2\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\kappa)|\kappa|.

(5.184)

Then choosing
\kappa=\varrho^{2}F^{k}
in (
5.184
) and using (
5.181
) lead to

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{6}|F^{k}|^{2}\big)=&\varrho^{6}\big\{-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|F^{k}|^{2}+2\big(-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)|F^{k}|^{2}+2e^{k}\cdot F^{k}\\
&+2\big(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}\big)\not{d}Z^{k}E\cdot F^{k}-2\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\cdot F^{k}\big\}.\end{split}

This yields,

\begin{split}|\mathring{L}(\varrho^{3}|F^{k}|)|\lesssim&\varrho^{3}|\check{\chi}|\cdot|F^{k}|+\varrho^{3}|-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}|\cdot|F^{k}|+\varrho^{3}|-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c\\
&+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}|\cdot|\not{d}Z^{k}E|+\varrho^{3}|\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})|+\varrho^{3}|e^{k}|.\end{split}

(5.185)

Then it follows from this and (
4.166
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\varrho^{3}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{l}\|F^{k}(t_{0},\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\varrho^{1/2}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\Big\{\tau^{5/2}\|\check{\chi}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\cdot\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\tau^{5/2}\|-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\cdot\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\tau^{5/2}\|-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\cdot\|\not{d}Z^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\tau^{5/2}\|\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{5/2}\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big\}d\tau.\end{split}

Thus, the Gronwall’s inequality yields

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\varrho^{5/2}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{l}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\Big\{&\tau^{3/2}\|\not{d}Z^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{5/2}\|\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\tau^{5/2}\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big\}{d}\tau.\end{split}

(5.186)

Each term in the integrand of (
5.186
) will be estimated as follows.

First, due to
E=c^{-1}\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-\frac{3}{2}c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta}
, then

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k}E\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-2}+\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},\end{split}

(5.187)

where one has used
L^{\infty}
estimates in Section
3
, Proposition
4.1
and Lemma
4.4
.

Next, direct computations give

\begin{split}\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})=&2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi)+2\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k,\ k_{1}\leq k-1}Z^{k_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(\not{d}Z^{k_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\\
=&2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(F^{k}+\not{d}Z^{k}E)+2\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k,\ k_{1}\leq k-1}Z^{k_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(\not{d}Z^{k_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}).\end{split}

(5.188)

Taking
L^{2}
norm of (
5.188
) on the surfaces
\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}
directly, and
applying the estimates in Section
3
and Section
4
to handle the lower and higher order derivatives respectively,
one can deduce

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.189)

Finally, it remains to treat
e^{k}
.
One starts with the term
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},Z]}F^{k_{2}}

in (
5.182
) with
k_{1}+k_{2}=k-1
. If
Z\in\{R,T\}
, then
[\mathring{L},Z]={\vphantom{{(Z)}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X}X

by (
2.78
), and therefore,
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},Z]}F^{k_{2}}={\vphantom{{(Z)}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\cdot\not{\nabla}F^{k_{2}}+F^{k_{2}}\cdot\not{\nabla}{\vphantom{{(Z)}}}^{(Z)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}
. This leads to that by Proposition
4.1
,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},Z]}F^{k_{2}}&\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.190)

If
Z=\varrho\mathring{L}
, then by
[\mathring{L},Z]=\mathring{L}
and (
5.181
),

\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{[\mathring{L},Z]}F^{k_{2}}=\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\big\{(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma})\not{d}Z^{k_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi-\not{d}Z^{k_{2}}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})+e^{k_{2}}\big\}.

(5.191)

Note that the estimate of
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k_{1}}\not{d}Z^{k_{2}}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})

in (
5.191
) follows from Proposition
4.1
immediately as,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k-1}(|\check{\chi}|^{2})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{5/2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.192)

The other two terms in (
5.191
) can be estimated similarly as for the remaining terms in (
5.182
) as below.

Indeed, for the term
Z^{k-k_{1}}(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta})\not{d}Z^{k_{1}}\textrm{tr}\chi

in (
5.182
) with
k_{1}\leq k-1
, Proposition
4.1
implies

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k-k_{1}}(-\frac{2}{\varrho}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\beta}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\beta})\not{d}Z^{k_{1}}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}+\mathfrak{t}^{-2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-3}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.193)

To estimate
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}e^{0}
in (
5.182
),
by (
5.183
), (
5.1
) and Proposition
4.1
, one has

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}e^{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}+\mathfrak{t}^{-2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}s+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-7/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.194)

Combining the estimates (
5.190
)-(
5.194
) and (
5.182
) yields

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|e^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\||_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}+\mathfrak{t}^{-2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}\\
&+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-3}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.195)

By inserting (
5.187
), (
5.189
) and (
5.195
) into (
5.186
), one obtains from (
5.175
) and (
5.176
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|F^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-2}+\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.196)

In addition, due to the definition of
F^{k}
,
\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi=F^{k}+\not{d}Z^{k}E
, and hence it holds that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\|\not{\textrm{div}}{\not{\mathcal{L}}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-2}+\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},\end{split}

(5.197)

where one has used (
5.196
) and (
5.187
).

Remark 5.1
.

Since
\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}=\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi

by (
2.52
), (
5.197
) gives also the
L^{2}
estimate for
\not{d}Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}
.

5.2
Estimates for the derivatives of
\not{\triangle}\mu

As in Subsection
5.1
, one can use (
2.58
) to estimate
\not{\triangle}\mu
.
Indeed, it follows from
[\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]\mu=-2(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\not{\triangle}\mu-2\varrho^{-1}\not{\triangle}\mu-\not{d}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\cdot\not{d}\mu
due to Lemma
2.8

and (
2.58
) that

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\not{\triangle}\mu=&[\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]\mu+\not{\triangle}\mathring{L}\mu\\
=&-2(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\not{\triangle}\mu-\frac{2}{\varrho}\not{\triangle}\mu-{\not{d}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}}\cdot\not{d}\mu+(\not{\triangle}\mu)c^{-1}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mathring{L}c)+2\not{d}\mu\cdot\not{d}(c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}\\
&-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c)+\mu\big\{\uwave{\not{\triangle}(c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\alpha})}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}+2\not{d}(c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\alpha})\cdot\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}\big\}+\uline{\mathring{L}(c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\alpha})}\\
&-\mathring{L}(c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha})\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\alpha}-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha}[\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]\varphi_{\alpha}+\mu[\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]\ln c-\underline{\mathring{L}(\mu\not{\triangle}\ln c)}+(\mathring{L}\mu)\not{\triangle}\ln c.\end{split}

(5.198)

To estimate the term with wavy line in (
5.198
), one notes that (
2.65
) implies

\begin{split}\not{\triangle}\check{L}^{a}=&\not{d}\textrm{tr}\chi\cdot\not{d}x^{a}+\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\not{\triangle}x^{a}+\not{\nabla}^{X}\big\{\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}(\mathring{L}c)\not{d}_{X}x^{a}-\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}(\not{d}_{X}c)\tilde{T}^{a}+c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{b}(\not{d}_{X}\varphi_{b})\tilde{T}^{a}\big\},\end{split}

(5.199)

and the
L^{2}
norm of each term in (
5.199
)
can be estimated by (
5.197
).

Observe that the two terms with the underline in (
5.198
) are both derivatives with respect
to
\mathring{L}
, and then can be moved to the left hand side of (
5.198
). Let
\tilde{E}=c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mu\not{\triangle}\ln c
and
\tilde{F}=\not{\triangle}\mu-\tilde{E}
.
Then (
5.198
) can be rewritten as

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\tilde{F}=&(-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-2\varrho^{-1}+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c)\tilde{F}\\
&-\not{d}\textrm{tr}\chi\cdot(\not{d}\mu-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}\not{d}x^{a}-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\not{d}\varphi_{\alpha}+\mu\not{d}\ln c)+\tilde{e}.\end{split}

Let
\bar{Z}
be any vector field in
\{T,R\}
and set
\bar{F}^{k}=\bar{Z}^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu-\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{E}
.
Then one can get by induction as for (
5.181
) that

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\bar{F}^{k}=&(-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-2\varrho^{-1}+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c)\bar{F}^{k}\\
&-\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\cdot(\not{d}\mu-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}\not{d}x^{a}-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\not{d}\varphi_{\alpha}+\mu\not{d}\ln c)+[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]^{X}\not{d}_{X}\bar{F}^{k-1}+\bar{e}^{k}\end{split}

(5.200)

with

\begin{split}\bar{e}^{k}=&\sum_{{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}k_{1}+k_{2}=k-1\\
|k_{1}|\geq 1\end{array}$}}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]^{X}\not{d}_{X}\bar{F}^{k_{2}}+\bar{Z}^{k}\tilde{e}+\sum_{{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{c}k_{1}+k_{2}=k\\
|k_{1}|\geq 1\end{array}$}}}\Big\{\bar{Z}^{k_{1}}(-2\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-2\varrho^{-1}+c^{-1}\mathring{L}^{\gamma}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}-c^{-1}\mathring{L}c)\bar{F}^{k_{2}}\\
&-(\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}\textrm{tr}\chi)\cdot\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{k_{1}}(\not{d}\mu-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}\not{d}x^{a}-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\not{d}\varphi_{\alpha}+\mu\not{d}\ln c)\Big\},\end{split}

where the first sum on the right hand side above vanishes when
k=1
. Thus, applying (
4.166
) to
F(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)=\varrho^{2}\bar{F}^{k}(\mathfrak{t},u,\vartheta)-\varrho_{0}^{2}\bar{F}^{k}(t_{0},u,\vartheta)

and using (
5.200
) lead to

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\varrho^{3/2}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\delta^{l}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{1/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{3/2}\delta^{l}\|\bar{e}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau.\end{split}

(5.201)

It follows from (
5.197
) and Proposition
4.1
that

\begin{split}&\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\delta^{1+l-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{1/2}\|\not{d}\bar{Z}^{k}\textrm{tr}\chi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{3/2}\delta^{l}\|\bar{e}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\biggl(\tau^{-1/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}+\delta\tau^{-3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)}\biggr)d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.202)

Inserting (
5.202
) into (
5.201
) and appying the Gronwall’s inequality yield

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\varrho^{3/2}\|\bar{F}^{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},\end{split}

and hence,

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|\bar{Z}^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}+\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

The other cases, which contain at least one
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{k}
, can be treated by using (
2.58
) and commutators
[\varrho\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]
and
[\varrho\mathring{L},\not{\triangle}]
.
Therefore, we eventually arrive at

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}\not{\triangle}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}+\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.203)

At the end of this section, we are going to improve the
L^{2}
estimate of
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}

in (
4.169
), which
was obtained by integrating
\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{k}\check{\chi}
along integral curves
of
\mathring{L}
. Such an approach caused losses of time decay of some related terms. To avoid such a difficulty, we
now use (
5.197
) and (
5.203
) and carry out
L^{2}
-estimate directly to the equation of
\check{\chi}
under actions of different
vector fields.

Corollary 5.1
.

Under the assumptions
(\star)
with
\delta>0
small, it then holds that for
k\leq 2N-6
,

\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},

(5.204)

where
l
is the number of
T
in the corresponding derivatives. Furthermore,

\delta^{l}\|(\mathfrak{t}Z^{k}\mathring{L}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi},\delta Z^{k}T\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\mathfrak{t}^{-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.

(5.205)

Proof.

We first derive (
5.204
).
Without loss of generality,
k\geq 1
is assumed.

Z^{k-1}(\varrho\mathring{L})\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}
,
Z^{k-1}T\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}

and
Z^{k-1}R\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}
will be treated separately as follows.

First, it follows from (
3.86
) that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}|Z^{k-1}(\varrho\mathring{L})\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\lesssim&\delta^{l_{1}}|Z^{n_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}x|+\delta^{l_{2}}|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{2}}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{l_{2}}|\not{d}Z^{n_{2}}\varphi|+\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\varphi|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}|Z^{n_{1}}\check{L}^{i}|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|,\end{split}

(5.206)

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}

(i=0,1,2)
, and
n_{i}\leq k-2+i
. (
5.206
) and Proposition
4.1
imply

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k-1}(\varrho\mathring{L})\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\mathfrak{t}^{-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)},\end{split}

(5.207)

which, together with (
4.165
), yields

\delta^{l}\|Z^{k-1}(\varrho\mathring{L})\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.

(5.208)

Next, thanks to (
3.87
), one has

\begin{split}\delta^{l+1}|Z^{k-1}T\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}|Z^{n_{1}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\delta^{1_{1}}|Z^{n_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|+\delta^{l_{1}+1}|Z^{n_{1}}\not{\triangle}\mu|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}x|+\delta^{l_{2}}|\not{d}Z^{n_{2}}\varphi|+\delta^{l_{2}}|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{2}}\varphi|+\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}|Z^{n_{1}}\check{L}^{i}|+\delta t^{-2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\mu|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|,\end{split}

(5.209)

where the number of
T
in
Z^{k-1}
is
l
,
l_{i}
and
n_{i}

(i=0,1,2)
are given as in (
5.206
).
One now applies (
5.203
) to estimate
Z^{n_{1}}\not{\triangle}\mu
and uses Proposition
4.1
to
handle the other terms in (
5.209
). This leads to

\delta^{l+1}\|Z^{k-1}T\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\mathfrak{t}^{-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.

(5.210)

Thus

\delta^{l+1}\|Z^{k-1}T\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.

(5.211)

Finally, due to
Z^{k-1}R\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}=[Z^{k-1},R]\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}+RZ^{k-1}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}
, then it holds that

\delta^{l}|Z^{k-1}R\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{0}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{0}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{T}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|+\mathfrak{t}\delta^{l_{1}}|\not{d}Z^{n_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|,

which, together with Proposition
4.1
, (
5.197
) and (
4.165
), implies

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k-1}R\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}&\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\mathfrak{t}^{-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+1}(\mathfrak{t},u)}\\
&\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{E_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(5.212)

Collecting (
5.208
), (
5.211
) and (
5.212
) leads to the desired estimate (
5.204
).
Moreover, (
5.205
) follows from (
5.207
) and (
5.210
) directly.
∎

6
Estimates for the error terms

With the
L^{2}
estimates in Sections
4
and
5
,
we are ready to handle the error terms
\delta\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|\Phi\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\Psi|

and
|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|
in (
4.146
), and then
get the final energy estimates for
\varphi
.
For the 4D case in
[
11
]
, the time decay rate of the solution is
\varrho^{-3/2}
,
which leads to the direct treatments for the term
|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|

with
m\in(0,1/2)
. However, as explained in Subsection
1.2.1
, here the 2D case is rather different due to the slower time decay.
To estimate
\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)

and its top order derivatives,
one needs the following two lemmas.

Lemma 6.1
.

For any smooth functions
f
and
h
, it holds that

\displaystyle\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}(fh)=

\displaystyle\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\{\mathring{L}(fh)+\textrm{tr}\chi(fh)\}+\int_{S_{t_{0},u}}(fh)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}f(\mathring{L}h+\frac{1}{2\varrho}h)+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}(\mathring{L}f+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f)h+\int_{C_{u}^{\mathfrak{t}}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}(fh)+\int_{S_{t_{0},u}}(fh),

(6.213)

\displaystyle\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}(Rf)h=

\displaystyle-\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}f(Rh)-\frac{1}{2}\int_{S_{\mathfrak{t},u}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(fh).

(6.214)

Proof.

This follows from similar arguments in
[
28
]
.
∎

Lemma 6.2
.

If
f_{i}

(i=1,2,3)
are smooth functions, then it holds that

\begin{split}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{f_{1}\cdot(\mathring{L}f_{2}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{2})\cdot Rf_{3}\}=&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{f_{1}\cdot Rf_{2}\cdot\mathring{L}f_{3}\}+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\{f_{1}\cdot Rf_{2}\cdot f_{3}\}\\
&+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Er_{1}+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}Er_{2}+\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}Er_{3}\end{split}

(6.215)

with

\displaystyle Er_{1}=

\displaystyle(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot Rf_{3}-Rf_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot\mathring{L}f_{3}-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot\mathring{L}f_{3}-f_{1}\cdot({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\not{d}^{X}f_{2})\cdot f_{3}

\displaystyle-({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\not{d}^{X}f_{1})\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}-(\not{\textrm{div}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}-(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})f_{1}\cdot Rf_{2}\cdot f_{3}

\displaystyle-R(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3},

\displaystyle Er_{2}=

\displaystyle Rf_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}+\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3},

\displaystyle Er_{3}=

\displaystyle-f_{1}\cdot Rf_{2}\cdot f_{3}-Rf_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}-\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}.

Proof.

Let
f=f_{1}\cdot Rf_{3}
and
h=f_{2}
in (
6.213
) and then integrate over
[0,u]
to get

\begin{split}&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{f_{1}\cdot(\mathring{L}f_{2}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{2})\cdot Rf_{3}\}\\
=&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot Rf_{3}\}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})(f_{1}\cdot Rf_{3})\cdot f_{2}\}\\
&-\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot Rf_{3}\}+\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot Rf_{3}\}\\
:=&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot Rf_{3}\}+I+II+III.\end{split}

(6.216)

Choosing
f=f_{3}
and
h=f_{2}(\mathring{L}f_{1}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{1})
in (
6.214
) yields

\begin{split}I=&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}R\{f_{2}(\mathring{L}f_{1}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{1})\}f_{3}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\{f_{2}(\mathring{L}f_{1}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{1})f_{3}\}\\
&+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot R\mathring{L}f_{3}\}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\not{d}^{X}f_{3})\}.\end{split}

(6.217)

Treating
\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot R\mathring{L}f_{3}\}
by using (
6.214
), one can get from (
6.217
) that

\begin{split}I=&-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Rf_{2}(\mathring{L}f_{1}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{1})f_{3}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}f_{2}\{R(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})f_{1}\}f_{3}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}(\not{\textrm{div}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\{f_{2}(\mathring{L}f_{1}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}f_{1})f_{3}\}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}f_{1}\cdot Rf_{2}\cdot\mathring{L}f_{3}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Rf_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot\mathring{L}f_{3}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\{f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot\mathring{L}f_{3}\}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}f_{1}({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\not{d}^{X}f_{2})f_{3}-\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}X}\not{d}^{X}f_{1})f_{2}\cdot f_{3}.\end{split}

(6.218)

Similarly,

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}f_{1}(Rf_{2})f_{3}+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}Rf_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3},

(6.219)

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}f_{1}(Rf_{2})f_{3}-\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}Rf_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})f_{1}\cdot f_{2}\cdot f_{3}.

(6.220)

Thus (
6.215
) follows from substituting (
6.218
)-(
6.220
) into (
6.216
).
∎

Recall the notations in Section
4
that for
\Psi=Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}=Z_{k+1}Z_{k}\cdots Z_{1}\varphi_{\gamma}
,
\Phi=\Phi_{\gamma}^{k+1}\equiv J_{1}^{k}+J_{2}^{k}
is given explicitly in (
4.149
) with
J_{1}^{k}
and
J_{2}^{k}
being the summation and the rest in (
4.149
) respectively. We will also use the notation that
\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}=Z_{n}\cdots Z_{1}\varphi_{\gamma}
for
n\geq 1
, and
\varphi_{\gamma}^{0}=\varphi_{\gamma}
. Then the main task is to estimate
J_{1}^{k}
and
J_{2}^{k}
.

6.1
Estimates for
J_{1}^{k}

It follows from the explicit form of
J_{1}^{k}
that the key is to estimate the derivatives of
\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z)}C_{\gamma}^{n}

(0\leq n\leq k)
. Due to (
4.153
) and (
4.154
)-(
4.156
), the treatment involving
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}
will be given separately from those for
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}
and
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}
, since the latter do not contain the top order derivatives of
\varphi_{\gamma}
.

(1)

We start with the estimates involving
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}
and
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}
. Substituting (
2.59
)-(
2.61
) into (
4.154
) and (
4.156
) yields

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=&T\mu\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\mu(\not{d}\mu+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\cdot\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})\\
&+(\not{d}\mu+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\cdot\not{d}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-T\mu\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\big(-c^{-1}Tc-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}c\\
&+2c^{-1}\mu\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-2\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(6.221)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=&\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi T\mu+(\frac{1}{4}\mu\textrm{tr}\chi+\frac{1}{2}\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta})\textrm{tr}_{\not{g}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}-\frac{1}{2}|\not{d}\mu|^{2}-c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\mu\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\qquad\qquad\\
&+(\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu-\mu\textrm{tr}\chi)(\not{d}\mu+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(6.222)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=&(2-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu)\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-2\varrho(\not{d}\mu+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\cdot\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\varrho\textrm{tr}\chi(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})+\varrho(\mu\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-\mathring{L}\mu)\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\end{split}

(6.223)
