\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=&\textrm{tr}\chi\big\{2-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu+\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\qquad\quad\\
&+2\varrho\textrm{tr}\chi(\not{d}\mu+2\mu c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(6.224)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}=&R\mu\mathring{L}^{2}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\cdot\not{d}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(\mathring{L}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n})\qquad\qquad\qquad\\
&-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\cdot\not{d}\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-R\mu\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\mu(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi})\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(6.225)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,3}^{n}=&\big\{\textrm{tr}\chi R\mu+\frac{1}{2}\mu(\textrm{tr}\tilde{\theta}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi)\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}+\frac{1}{2}\not{d}\mu\cdot{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\big\{\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(\not{d}\mu\\
&+2\mu c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})-\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\mu{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}+\frac{1}{2}\mu\textrm{tr}\chi{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\big\}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}.\end{split}

(6.226)

Note that
\mathring{L}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}\chi\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}
appears in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,1}^{n}
and
J_{1}^{k}
contains at most the
(k-n)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,i}^{n}

(i=1,3)
. Then it can be checked that the
L^{2}
norms of all the terms involving
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,i}^{n}

(i=1,3)
in
J_{1}^{k}
can be treated by using the
L^{\infty}
-estimates in Section
3
and the related
L^{2}
estimates of Proposition
4.1
.
Therefore, it holds that

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-j}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\|\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-j}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&\qquad\cdot\|\mathring{\underline{L}}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.227)

Similarly, one can get that

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,3}^{k-j}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.228)

The corresponding terms in
\delta^{2l}|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\Phi(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\Psi+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\Psi)|

can also be estimated as

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mid\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big)\big({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-j}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,3}^{k-j}\big)(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}\Big\{\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big)\big({\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,1}^{k-j}\\
&\qquad+{\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,3}^{k-j}\big)\Big\}^{2}+\delta^{2l-1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|(\varrho^{m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3+2m}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.229)

(2)

We now estimate the terms involving
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}

(0\leq n\leq k)
in
J_{1}^{k}
. Note that in the special case
n=0
,
j
equals
k
in
J_{1}^{k}
, and the order of the top derivatives in
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
is
k
, so that
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
contains terms involving the
(k+1)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of the deformation tensor.
This prevents one from using Proposition
4.1
to estimate the
L^{2}
norm of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}

directly since the
L^{2}
norm of the
(k+1)^{\text{th}}
order derivatives of the deformation tensor can be controlled only by
\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+3}(\mathfrak{t},u)
and
\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+3}(\mathfrak{t},u)
in the energy estimates. Thus, we will
examine carefully the expression of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}
and apply the estimates in Section
5

to deal with the top order derivatives of
\textrm{tr}\chi
and
\mu
.
Indeed, it follows from direct computations that

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=&\mathring{L}T\mu\cdot\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-\frac{1}{2}\big(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\big)\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\boxed{\not{\textrm{div}}(\not{d}\mu}+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\underline{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\big\{\frac{1}{4}\mathring{\underline{L}}\big(-c^{-1}Tc-c^{-1}\mu\mathring{L}c+2\mu c^{-1}\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}\uwave{-2\mu\textrm{tr}\chi}\big)+\frac{1}{2}\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{X}\big(\mu\not{d}^{X}\mu}\\
&+2c^{-1}\mu^{2}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a}\big)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-\big\{\uline{\not{d}T\mu-\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}(\not{d}\mu}+2c^{-1}\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\big\}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&-\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{d}\big(2\mu^{2}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}}+2\varrho^{-1}\mu^{2}+c^{-1}\mu Tc+c^{-1}\mu^{2}\mathring{L}c-2c^{-1}\mu^{2}(\not{d}x^{a})\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}\big)\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\frac{1}{4}\big(\mathring{L}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}\big)\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(6.230)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=&\big\{\mathring{L}\big(-\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu\big)+\uwave{\frac{1}{2}\mathring{\underline{L}}\big(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)}-\boxed{\not{\nabla}_{X}\big(\varrho\not{d}^{X}\mu}+2c^{-1}\varrho\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{a}\big)\big\}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\big)\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-\big\{\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\big(\varrho\not{d}\mu+2c^{-1}\varrho\mu\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a}\big)+\not{d}\big(\mu+\varrho\mathring{L}\mu\big)\big\}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\underbrace{\not{d}\big(\varrho\mu\textrm{tr}\chi\big)}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n},\end{split}

(6.231)

\begin{split}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{n}=&(\mathring{L}R\mu)\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-\big\{\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}\big({\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\big)+\boxed{\not{d}R\mu}-\frac{1}{2}\uwave{\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\big(R^{X}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\not{g}}-g_{ab}\epsilon_{i}^{a}\check{L}^{i}\not{d}x^{b}\\
&-\upsilon c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}R^{X}\mathring{L}c\not{g}+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}\upsilon\not{d}c\big)\big\}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{4}\mathring{L}\big(\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\big)\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\frac{1}{4}\uwave{\mathring{\underline{L}}\big(2\upsilon\textrm{tr}\chi}+c^{-1}\upsilon\mathring{L}c-2c^{-1}\upsilon\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-c^{-1}Rc\big)\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}-\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{\nabla}_{X}\big(\mu R^{X}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}}\\
&+g_{ab}\mu\epsilon_{i}^{b}\check{L}^{i}\not{d}^{X}x^{a}+2g_{ab}\mu{\epsilon_{i}^{a}}\check{T}^{i}\not{d}x^{b}+\mu\upsilon c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}\mu R^{X}\mathring{L}c\\
&+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}\mu\upsilon\not{d}c+2\upsilon\not{d}\mu-2c^{-1}\mu R\varphi_{a}\not{d}^{X}x^{a}\big)\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}+\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{\nabla}_{X}\big(R^{X}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}}\\
&-g_{ab}\epsilon_{i}^{a}\check{L}^{i}\not{d}^{X}x^{b}-\upsilon c^{-1}\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}^{X}\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}R^{X}\mathring{L}c+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}\upsilon\not{d}_{X}c\big)\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\underbrace{\not{d}\big(2\upsilon\mu\textrm{tr}\chi}+c^{-1}\mu\upsilon\mathring{L}c-2c^{-1}\mu\upsilon\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-c^{-1}\mu Rc\big)\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}.\end{split}

(6.232)

It is emphasized that special attentions are needed for terms with underlines, wavy lines, boxes, or braces in (
6.230
)-(
6.232
). In (
6.230
),
due to
\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\not{d}\mu=\not{d}T\mu+\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}\mathring{L}\mu
, the
corresponding underline part is

\not{d}T\mu-\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\not{d}\mu=-\frac{1}{2}\mu\not{d}\mathring{L}\mu,

(6.233)

which can be estimated by using (
2.58
). For the terms with wavy lines in (
6.230
)-(
6.232
), one can use (
3.87
) and (
3.86
) to replace
\frac{1}{2}\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\check{\chi}
by
\not{\nabla}^{2}\mu+\cdots
which can be handled by (
5.203
).
We also apply (
5.203
) and (
5.197
) to estimate those terms with boxes and braces respectively.
Meanwhile, one notes that in (
6.230
)-(
6.232
), there are some terms containing derivatives of the deformation tensors with respect to
\mathring{L}
, for example,
\frac{1}{2}\big(\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\mathring{L}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{\underline{L}}}\big)\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{\gamma}^{n}
appears in (
6.230
). However, these terms can be estimated by taking into account of (
2.58
), (
3.86
), (
2.59
) and (
2.61
).

In summary, we can arrive at

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\\
&\qquad\qquad\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-1/2-m}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-1/2-m}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.234)

It remains to deal with

\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(Z_{k+1-j})}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid,

where
j=1,2,\cdots k
, which will be estimated as follows.

(a)

For
Z_{k+1-j}=T
, according (
6.230
), one can get from (
5.197
), (
5.203
) and Proposition
4.1
that

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\cdot(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}\mid\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(T)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\mid^{2}\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{6-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.235)

(b)

For
Z_{k+1-j}=\varrho\mathring{L}
,
Z^{k}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}

contains a term
\frac{1}{2}\big(Z^{k}\mathring{L}(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
.
Then (
3.86
) implies that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\mathring{L}(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})|&\lesssim\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|+\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\big(\varrho(\not{\triangle}\varphi_{0}+\mathring{L}^{a}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{a})\big)|+\delta^{l_{3}}|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}x|+\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\check{L}^{i}|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|,\end{split}

(6.236)

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}
and
n_{i}\leq k-2+i

(i=1,2,3)
. Due to (
5.174
),

\begin{split}\mu(\not{\triangle}\varphi_{0}+\mathring{L}^{a}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{a})=&\mathring{L}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi^{\alpha})-(\mathring{L}\mathring{L}^{a})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\underline{\mathring{L}}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\tilde{H}_{\alpha}.\end{split}

(6.237)

Substituting (
2.62
) and (
3.82
) (note that
\tilde{H}_{\alpha}=H_{\alpha}-\mu\not{\triangle}\varphi_{\alpha})

into (
6.237
) yields

\begin{split}&\not{\triangle}\varphi_{0}+\mathring{L}^{a}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{a}\\
=&(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})\mathring{L}\varphi_{0}+\sum_{a=1}^{2}\big\{(2\varphi_{a}-\mathring{L}^{a})(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}+\mathring{L}(2\varphi_{a}-\mathring{L}^{a})\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}\big\}\\
&+c^{-1}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mathring{L}c)\big\{\mathring{L}\varphi_{0}+\sum_{a=1}^{2}(2\varphi_{a}-\mathring{L}^{a})\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}\big\}+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}(\tilde{T}^{a}\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}\\
&-\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-c\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-c\varrho^{-1})\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}+c^{-1}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\not{d}\varphi_{\alpha}+3\tilde{T}^{a}\not{d}\varphi_{a})\cdot\not{d}x^{b}(\mathring{L}\varphi_{b})\\
&+\frac{1}{2}c^{-1}(\mathring{L}c+3\tilde{T}^{a}\mathring{L}\varphi_{a}-3\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}+c\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}+2\tilde{T}^{\alpha})\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}.\end{split}

(6.238)

It then follows from (
6.238
) and Proposition
4.1
that
for any fixed constant
\backepsilon\geq\frac{1}{2}
,

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-\backepsilon}|Z^{k}\big(\varrho(\not{\triangle}\varphi_{0}+\mathring{L}^{a}\not{\triangle}\varphi_{a})\big)|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l_{3}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-\backepsilon}|(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}+\delta^{2l_{3}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-2-\backepsilon}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}+2l_{2}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-3-\backepsilon}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{2}}\check{L}^{i}|^{2}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}+l_{3}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-5-\backepsilon}|Z^{n_{3}}x|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}+l_{2}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-1-\backepsilon}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|^{2}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}+2l_{1}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-3-\backepsilon}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}+2l_{1}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-3-\backepsilon}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-1-\backepsilon}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-2-\backepsilon}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.239)

In addition, (
4.164
) implies that for the constant
\backepsilon\geq\frac{1}{2}
,

\begin{split}&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-\backepsilon}\delta^{2l_{3}}|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-\backepsilon}\delta^{2l_{3}}|(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-2-\backepsilon}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,n_{3}+1}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{2}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2-\backepsilon}E_{1,n_{3}+1}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{2m-2-\backepsilon}E_{2,n_{3}+1}.\end{split}

(6.240)

On the other hand, applying (
5.204
), (
6.239
) and (
6.240
) to estimate the first line at the right hand side of (
6.236
),
and utilizing (
4.164
) and Proposition
4.1
to handle the other terms, one then can obtain by
choosing
\backepsilon=1
in (
6.239
) and (
6.240
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}|Z^{k}\mathring{L}(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})|^{2}|\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}|^{2}\lesssim\delta^{2l-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}|Z^{k}\mathring{L}(\varrho\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.241)

Therefore, thanks to (
6.231
), Proposition
4.1
and (
6.241
), one has

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\cdot(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m}\mid\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(\varrho\mathring{L})}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\mid^{2}\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\end{split}

\begin{split}\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.242)

(c)

Finally, we deal with the most difficult case,
Z_{k+1-j}=R
. In this case, it follows from (
6.232
) that
Z^{k}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}
contains the term
\frac{1}{2}(RZ^{k}\text{tr}\check{\chi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
whose treatment is more subtle and will be given later in Proposition
6.1
. The rest can be estimated as follows

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}|Z^{k}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}(RZ^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\underbrace{\delta^{l_{3}}|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|}_{\eqref{LZphi}}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\underbrace{\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\mathring{L}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|}_{\eqref{ZLch}}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\underbrace{\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|}_{\eqref{uZkchi}}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-2}\delta^{l_{2}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}^{n_{2}}_{Z}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\mu|+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}\delta^{l_{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{3}}\check{L}^{i}|\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{2}}\check{L}^{i}|+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}\delta^{l_{4}}|Z^{n_{4}}x|+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\upsilon|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{3}}|\underline{\mathring{L}}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\delta^{l_{3}}|\not{d}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}\delta^{l_{2}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\upsilon|\\
&+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\underbrace{\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}T\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|}_{\eqref{ZTchi}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\underbrace{\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\not{\triangle}\mu|}_{\eqref{Zmu}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\underbrace{\delta^{l_{2}}|\not{d}Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|}_{\eqref{d}},\end{split}

(6.243)

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}
and
n_{i}\leq k-2+i

(i=2,3,4)
.
To estimate the
L^{2}
-norm of
\delta^{l}|Z^{k}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}(RZ^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}|
over
D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}
, one can bound the
L^{2}
-norms of the terms underlined with braces in (
6.243
) by the corresponding estimates indicated bellow the braces.
While the other terms without braces can be treated by using Proposition
4.1
and (
4.164
). Then one can conclude that

\begin{split}&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\big(Z^{k}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}-\frac{1}{2}(RZ^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}\big)\cdot(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.244)

Finally, it remains to treat
\frac{1}{2}(RZ^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
, whose
L^{2}
norm cannot be estimated by (
5.197
) directly since the resulting time decay rate is not enough to close the energy estimate (
4.146
) (see also the beginning of Section
7
). Our strategy here is based on the structural equation (
5.175
). Indeed, (
5.175
) implies that
\mathring{L}(\text{tr}\check{\chi}-E)
admits better rate of decay in time, which, combined with (
6.215
) and (
6.214
), will enable us to obtain the desired estimates for corresponding terms. Meanwhile, the terms involving
E
defined by (
5.176
) can be handled easily by using Proposition
4.1
and (
6.240
) directly. Thus we will show

Proposition 6.1
.

For
\delta>0
small, it holds that

\begin{split}&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}(RZ^{k}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{m-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u),\end{split}

(6.245)

where

\tilde{F}_{i,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)=\sup_{t_{0}\leq\tau\leq\mathfrak{t}}F_{i,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u),\quad i=1,2.

Proof.

Noting (
5.175
) for
\textrm{tr}\chi
, one can bound the left hand side of (
6.245
) by
|\bar{I}|+|\overline{II}|
with

\begin{split}&\bar{I}=\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}RZ^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\cdot\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}),\\
&\overline{II}=\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}(RZ^{k}E)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}),\end{split}

where
E
is defined in (
5.176
).

We start with the easy term
\overline{II}
. It is noted that
RZ^{k}E
contains
Z^{k}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a})
and

\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a}=\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})+(Rx^{a})\not{\triangle}\varphi_{a}.

Replacing
\not{\triangle}\varphi_{a}
with
\mu^{-1}(\mathring{L}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2\varrho}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{a}-\tilde{H}_{a})
and applying (
3.82
) yield

\begin{split}&\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a}\\
=&(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})R\varphi_{0}+\sum_{a=1}^{2}(2\varphi_{a}-\mathring{L}^{a})(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})R\varphi_{a}+\frac{1}{2}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a})\\
&-\sum_{a=1}^{2}(2\varphi_{a}-\mathring{L}^{a}){\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{0}-\frac{3}{2\varrho}R\varphi_{0}\\
&-\frac{1}{2\varrho}\sum_{a=1}^{2}(4\varphi_{a}-\mathring{L}^{a})R\varphi_{a}+c^{-1}f(\mathring{L}^{1},\mathring{L}^{2},\varphi)\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathring{L}\varphi\\
\not{d}x^{b}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{b}\\
\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\end{array}\right)R\varphi.\end{split}

(6.246)

It then follows from (
5.176
) and (
6.246
) that

\begin{split}|\overline{II}|\lesssim&\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-3}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-6}\delta^{2l_{4}}|Z^{n_{4}}x|^{2}+\delta^{2l+1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}|Z^{k}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a})|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{2}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}+\delta^{5-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-6}\delta^{2l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{5-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{3}}\check{L}^{i}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{2l+1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}|\mathring{L}Z^{k}R\varphi|^{2}\end{split}

\begin{split}\lesssim&\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}\delta^{2l}|(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})Z^{k}R\varphi|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-6}\delta^{2l_{4}}|Z^{n_{4}}x|^{2}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{3}}\check{L}^{i}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{2}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}+\delta^{5-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-6}\delta^{2l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{5-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-4}\delta^{2l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-3}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}\delta^{2l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{2l+1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-1}|\mathring{L}Z^{k}R\varphi|^{2},\end{split}

(6.247)

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}
and
n_{i}\leq k-2+i

(i=1,2,3,4)
.
Applying (
6.240
) with
\backepsilon=1
to estimate the last term and using Proposition
4.1
to
deal with the other corresponding terms, one then obtains

\begin{split}|\overline{II}|\lesssim&\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.248)

We now treat the difficult term
\bar{I}
. Choose
f_{1}=\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}
,
f_{2}=Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}
and
f_{3}=\varrho^{2}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)
in (
6.215
), and define

\begin{split}&\bar{I}_{1}=\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{2}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma}),\\
&\bar{I}_{2}=\delta^{2l}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})\big(\varrho^{2}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big).\end{split}

It then holds that

-\bar{I}=-\bar{I}_{1}+\bar{I}_{2}+\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Er_{1}+\delta^{2l}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}Er_{2}+\delta^{2l}\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}Er_{3}.

(6.249)

Note that (
5.175
) implies

\begin{split}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)=&-2\varrho E+c^{-1}\varrho^{2}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mathring{L}c)\textrm{tr}\chi-\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})^{2}+\varrho^{2}e\\
=&c^{-1}\varrho(-2\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}+2\mathring{L}c-\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})+c^{-1}\varrho^{2}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mathring{L}c)\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\\
&-\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})^{2}+\varrho^{2}e.\end{split}

(6.250)

Then
\bar{I}_{1}
can be rewritten as

\begin{split}\bar{I}_{1}=&\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}[\mathring{L},Z^{k}]\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\\
&+\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mathring{L}\big([\varrho^{2},Z^{k}](\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\\
&+\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}Z^{k}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\\
=:&\bar{I}_{11}+\bar{I}_{12}+\bar{I}_{13}.\end{split}

\bar{I}_{1i}
(
i=1,2,3
) will be treated separately as follows.

i.

For
\bar{I}_{11}
, one can use the facts that
[\mathring{L},Z^{k}]=\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}=k-1}Z^{k_{1}}[\mathring{L},Z]Z^{k_{2}}
,

[\mathring{L},\varrho\mathring{L}]=\mathring{L}
and
[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]={\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}^{X}\not{d}_{X}
with
\bar{Z}\in\{T,R\}
,
and Proposition
4.1
to obtain

\begin{split}&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Z^{k_{1}}[\mathring{L},\varrho\mathring{L}]Z^{k_{2}}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})Z^{k_{2}}R\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Z^{k_{1}}\mathring{L}Z^{k_{2}}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})([RZ^{k_{1}},\varrho\mathring{L}]Z^{k_{2}}R\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
&+\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Z^{k_{1}}\mathring{L}Z^{k_{2}}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(\varrho\mathring{L}RZ^{k_{1}}Z^{k_{2}}R\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\{\underbrace{\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|}+\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}E|\}\big\{\tau^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|\\
&\qquad\quad+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{1}}(|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|)+\underbrace{\varrho\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}Z^{k+1}\varphi|}\big\}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-5/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.251)

where the terms underlined by braces have been estimated by using (
5.204
), (
6.240
) with
\backepsilon=\frac{1}{2}
and (
4.165
). Similarly,

\begin{split}&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Z^{k_{1}}[\mathring{L},\bar{Z}]Z^{k_{2}}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{4m-4}\delta^{2l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|^{2}+\delta^{2-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{4m-2}\delta^{2l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{4-6\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{4m-7}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}x|^{2}+\delta^{2-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{4m-4}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{4-6\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{4m-5}\delta^{2l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{2}}\check{L}^{i}|^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{2l}|\not{d}Z^{k+1}\varphi|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-4}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{4m-4}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.252)

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}
with
n_{i}\leq k-2+i

(i=1,2,3)
.

Combining (
6.251
) with (
6.252
) yields

\begin{split}|\bar{I}_{11}|\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-5/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.253)

ii.

|\bar{I}_{12}|
can be handled similarly as in Case i. Indeed, due to
[\varrho^{2},\varrho\mathring{L}]f=-2\varrho^{2}f
and
[\varrho^{2},T]f=2\varrho f
, then
as for (
6.251
), one can get

\begin{split}&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mathring{L}\big(Z^{k_{1}}[\varrho^{2},\varrho\mathring{L}]Z^{k_{2}}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})Z^{k_{2}}R\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-5/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.254)

Meanwhile, it follows from (
5.204
) and Proposition
4.1
that

\begin{split}&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mathring{L}\big(Z^{k_{1}}[\varrho^{2},T]Z^{k_{2}}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)(\varrho^{2m-2}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma})(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{2l}|\not{d}Z^{k+1}\varphi|^{2}+\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\tau^{4m-3}\delta^{2l_{2}}\big(|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|^{2}+|Z^{n_{2}}E|^{2}\big)\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{5-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{4m-5}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau.\end{split}

(6.255)

Therefore,

\begin{split}|\bar{I}_{12}|\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-5/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.256)

iii.

One can use (
6.250
) to estimate
\bar{I}_{13}
.
Indeed, it follows from (
6.214
) and (
6.246
) that

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}Z^{k}\big(c^{-1}\varrho(2\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-2\mathring{L}c+\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\leq&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}RZ^{k}\big(c^{-1}\varrho(2\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-2\mathring{L}c+\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
&+\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}Z^{k}\big(c^{-1}\varrho(2\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-2\mathring{L}c+\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})\big)R\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}Z^{k}\big(c^{-1}\varrho(2\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-2\mathring{L}c+\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}\mid\\
\lesssim&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\big\{\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{4}}\tau^{-5/2}|Z^{n_{4}}x|+\tau^{-1}\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|+\delta^{l}|Z^{k}(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})R\varphi|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{2}}\tau^{-3/2}(|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}|+|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\delta|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(T)}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\tau|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|)\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{1}}\tau^{-5/2}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|+\underbrace{\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}RZ^{k}\varphi|}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{3}}\tau^{-3/2}|Z^{n_{3}}\check{L}^{i}|\big\}\delta^{l}|Z^{k+1}\varphi|,\end{split}

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}
with
n_{i}\leq k-2+i

(i=1,2,3,4)
, and the term underline with brace can be estimated by using (
6.240
) for
\backepsilon=\frac{1}{2}
.

This, together with Lemma
4.4
, Proposition
4.1
and (
6.240
), yields

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}Z^{k}\big(c^{-1}\varrho(2\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}\varphi_{a}-2\mathring{L}c+\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.257)

In addition, for
l_{i}
and
n_{i}
as in (
6.252
), direct computations yield

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\varrho^{2m-2}Z^{k}\big(c^{-1}\varrho^{2}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}-\mathring{L}c)\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi})^{2}+\varrho^{2}e\big)\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\mid\\
\lesssim&\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{2l}|\not{d}Z^{k+1}\varphi|^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{2-4\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{4m-4}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{2-4\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{4m-2}\delta^{2l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|^{2}\\
&+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{4-6\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{4m-7}\delta^{2l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}x|^{2}+\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\delta^{4-6\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{4m-5}\delta^{2l_{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{2}|Z^{n_{2}}L^{j}|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5-6\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-4}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{4m-4}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.258)

where the last integral in the second inequality has been estimated by (
5.204
).
Collecting (
6.250
), (
6.257
) and (
6.258
) yields

\begin{split}|\bar{I}_{13}|\lesssim&\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.259)

We conclude from (
6.253
), (
6.256
) and (
6.259
) that

\begin{split}|\bar{I}_{1}|\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.260)

To deal with
\bar{I}_{2}
, one can apply (
6.250
) to any vectorfield
\bar{Z}\in\{R,T\}
to get

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{2}R\bar{Z}^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)|\\
\leq&\delta^{l}|[\mathring{L},R\bar{Z}^{k}]\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)|+\delta^{l}|\mathring{L}R[\varrho^{2},\bar{Z}^{k}](\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)|+\delta^{l}|R\bar{Z}^{k}\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{2}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{1/2}\delta^{l_{2}}|R\bar{Z}^{n_{2}}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)|\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{2}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{\bar{Z}}^{n_{2}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{4}}|Z^{n_{4}}x|\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{1/2}\delta^{l_{3}}|\not{d}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}|\bar{Z}^{n_{3}}\check{L}^{i}|+\varrho\delta^{l_{2}}|\bar{Z}^{n_{2}}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a})|\\
&+\delta^{l_{3}}|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|+\mathfrak{t}\delta^{l_{3}}|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi|+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}|+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\delta^{l_{2}}|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|\\
&+\delta\varrho\delta^{l_{2}}|\bar{Z}^{n_{2}}\mathring{L}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}|.\end{split}

(6.261)

This, together with (
4.1
) and (
5.205
), yields

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|\mathring{L}\big(\varrho^{2}R\bar{Z}^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{1/2}\delta^{l}\|R\bar{Z}^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}\\
&+\delta\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\varrho\delta^{l_{2}}\|\bar{Z}^{n_{2}}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+t\delta^{l_{3}}\|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}.\end{split}

(6.262)

On the other hand, applying (
4.166
) to
F=\varrho^{2}R\bar{Z}^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)(t,u,\vartheta)-\varrho_{0}^{2}R\bar{Z}^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)(t_{0},u,\vartheta)
, one can get from (
6.262
) and (
6.246
) that

\begin{split}&\varrho^{3/2}\delta^{l}\|R\bar{Z}^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{1/2}\big\{\delta^{l_{2}}\|\bar{Z}^{n_{2}}(\not{d}x^{a}\cdot\not{d}R\varphi_{a})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{3}}\|\mathring{L}Z^{n_{3}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\big\}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{1/2}\delta^{l_{3}}\|(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})\bar{Z}^{n_{3}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}\\
\lesssim&\mathfrak{t}^{1-m}\Big(\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\Big)^{1/2}+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{1-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\\
\end{split}

(6.263)

When there is at least one
\varrho\mathring{L}
in
Z^{k}
, that is,
Z^{k}=Z^{k_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}
for
k_{1}+k_{2}=k-1
,
according to (
5.175
), Proposition
4.1
, (
5.197
) and (
4.165
), one can deduce that

\begin{split}&\delta^{l}\|RZ^{k_{1}}(\varrho\mathring{L})\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\leq&\delta^{l}\|RZ^{k_{1}}[\varrho\mathring{L},\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}](\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{l}\|RZ^{k_{1}}\bar{Z}^{k_{2}}(\varrho\mathring{L})(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{l_{1}}\|RZ^{n_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\delta^{l_{3}}\|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|Z^{n_{1}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{2}}\|Z^{n_{2}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2}\delta^{l_{3}}\|Z^{n_{3}}x^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}.\end{split}

(6.264)

Thus, it follows from Proposition
3.4
that

\begin{split}\delta^{l}\|RZ^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)&\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\lesssim\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2-m}\Big(\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\Big)^{1/2}+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2-m}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)}+\delta\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)},\end{split}

(6.265)

which, together with (
6.214
) and (
5.204
), yields

\begin{split}|\bar{I}_{2}|=&\delta^{2l}|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}(Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\varrho^{2m}R\big\{\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\big\}dx\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\varrho^{2m}(Z^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\cdot\textrm{tr}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}^{(R)}\not{\pi}dx|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-1/2}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\cdot\delta^{l}\|RZ^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-1/2}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\cdot\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}+\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{-1/2}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{-1/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-3/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u).\end{split}

(6.266)

Recall (
6.249
). It remains to estimate terms involving
Er_{i}

(i=1,2,3)
given in Lemma
6.2
. First, one has

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Er_{1}\mid\\
\lesssim&\delta^{-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-1}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big\{\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l}\|\not{d}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{-1/2}\delta^{l_{2}}\|Z^{n_{2}}\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-2}\delta^{l_{1}}\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{1}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{l_{3}}\|Z^{n_{3}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-2}\delta^{l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|Z^{n_{2}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\\
&+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3}\delta^{l_{3}}\|Z^{n_{3}}x\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\Big\}d\tau+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-1}\delta^{l}\|\not{d}Z^{k+1}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\delta^{l}\|Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}d\tau\\
&+\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\mid\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}\delta^{2l}(RZ^{k+1}\varphi_{\gamma})\varrho^{2m-1}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}Z^{k}(\textrm{tr}\check{\chi}-E)dx\mid d\tau,\end{split}

(6.267)

here one has used the identity
(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})f_{1}=(2m-2)\varrho^{2m-3}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+\varrho^{2m-2}(\mathring{L}+\frac{1}{2\varrho})\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}=(2m-2)\varrho^{2m-3}\underline{\mathring{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}+O(\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\varrho^{2m-4})
due to (
3.81
). Notice that the last term on
the right hand side of (
6.267
) is just
\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\varrho^{-1}|\bar{I}_{2}|d\tau
which can be estimated
by (
6.266
), while the other terms can be estimated by using (
5.197
), (
5.204
), (
4.164
) and Proposition
4.1
. Thus it holds that

\begin{split}\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}&Er_{1}\mid\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{m-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.268)

Next, it follows directly from (
5.204
) and Proposition
4.1
that

\delta^{2l}|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u}}Er_{2}|+\delta^{2l}|\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}^{u}}Er_{3}|\lesssim\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u).

(6.269)

Then the estimate for
\bar{I}
follows from (
6.260
), (
6.266
), (
6.268
), (
6.269
) and (
6.249
). This and (
6.248
)
complete the estimate (
6.245
) in Proposition
6.1
.
∎

Based on (
6.244
) and (
6.245
), we can end this subsection with

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}\mid\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\cdot(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid\\
\lesssim&\mid\delta^{2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}Z^{k}\ {\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{0}(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})\mid+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\sum_{p=0}^{k-1}\delta^{1+2l}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|Z^{p}\ {\vphantom{D}}{}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}|^{2}\cdot|Z^{\leq k-1-p}\ {\vphantom{\chi}}{}^{(Z)}\chi|^{2}\\
&+\delta^{1+2l}\sum_{j=1}^{k-1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\mid\sum_{j=1}^{k}\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{k+2-j}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+2-j})}\chi\big){\vphantom{D}}^{(R)}D_{\gamma,2}^{k-j}\mid^{2}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\tilde{F}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}\delta F_{2,k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\\
&+\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{m-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-5/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u).\end{split}

(6.270)

6.2
Estimates for
J_{2}^{k}

Recall that
J_{2}^{k}\equiv\mu\textrm{div}{\vphantom{C}}^{(Z_{k+1})}C_{\gamma}^{k}+\big(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi\big)\dots\big(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{1})}\chi\big)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}
does not contain
the top order derivatives of
\textrm{tr}{\chi}
and
\not{\triangle}\mu
. Therefore, according to
Proposition
4.1
and the expressions of
{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z)}D_{\gamma,j}^{k}
in (
6.221
)-(
6.226
) and (
6.230
)-(
6.232
),
one can get

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|\sum_{j=1}^{3}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z_{k+1})}D_{\gamma,j}^{k}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2m}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\end{split}

(6.271)

and

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}{\vphantom{D}}{}^{(Z_{k+1})}D_{\gamma,j}^{k}\cdot(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.272)

Note that
\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}=\mu\Box_{g}\varphi_{\gamma}
is given explicitly in (
2.77
).
Then, Proposition
4.1
and (
4.150
) lead to

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l+1}\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}|(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi)\cdots(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{1})}\chi)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}\cdot\mathring{\underline{L}}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{3-3\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{2-3\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2m-1/2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-3/2}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq k+2}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}\end{split}

(6.273)

and

\begin{split}&\delta^{2l}|\int_{D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}}(Z_{k+1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{k+1})}\chi)\cdots(Z_{1}+{\vphantom{\chi}}^{(Z_{1})}\chi)\Phi_{\gamma}^{0}\cdot(\varrho^{2m}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1}+\frac{1}{2}\varrho^{2m-1}\varphi_{\gamma}^{k+1})|\\
\lesssim&\delta^{4-4\varepsilon_{0}}+\delta^{3-4\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{-2}\tilde{E}_{1,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau+\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}\int_{t_{0}}^{\mathfrak{t}}\tau^{2m-3}\tilde{E}_{2,\leq k+2}(\tau,u)d\tau\\
&+\delta^{-1}\int_{0}^{u}F_{1,\leq n+1}(\mathfrak{t},u^{\prime})du^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.274)

7
Global estimates in
A_{2\delta}

Based on the estimates in Section
4
-
6
, we are now ready to prove the global uniform estimates of the smooth solution
\phi
to the equation (
1.8
) with
initial data (
1.2
) and (
1.9
)-(
1.10
) near
C_{0}
by following the framework in
[
11
]
. Furthermore, in order to estimate the solution inside
C_{0}
, we also need to estimate
\phi
on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
as in
[
11
]
. But thanks to the special structure of (
1.8
), it is easier to obtain
\mathring{L}\sim L
,
\mathring{L}+2T\sim\underline{L}
and
R\sim\Omega
than that in
[
11
]

(here the equivalence of two vectorfields means that there are same time decay rates and smallness orders when acting on the solution
\phi
).
Indeed, substituting (
6.227
)-(
6.229
), (
6.234
), (
6.235
), (
6.242
) and (
6.270
)-(
6.274
) into (
4.146
), and using the Gronwall’s inequality, one can get that under the assumptions
(\star)
with
small
\delta>0
for
\frac{1}{2}<m<\frac{3}{4}
,

\delta\tilde{E}_{2,\leq 2N-4}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\delta\tilde{F}_{2,\leq 2N-4}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\tilde{E}_{1,\leq 2N-4}(\mathfrak{t},u)+\tilde{F}_{1,\leq 2N-4}(\mathfrak{t},u)\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}.

(7.275)

Based on (
7.275
), we can close the bootstrap
assumptions
(\star)
in Section
3
. To this end, one needs the following Sobolev type embedding formula (see
[
28
, Proposition 18.10]
).

Lemma 7.1
.

For any function
f\in H^{2}(S_{{\mathfrak{t}},u})
, under the assumptions
(\star)
for
\delta>0
small, it holds that

\|f\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{{\mathfrak{t}},u})}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{t}}}\sum_{a\leq 1}\|R^{a}f\|_{L^{2}(S_{\mathfrak{t},u})}.

(7.276)

It follows from (
7.276
), (
7.275
) and (
4.161
) that for
k\leq 2N-6
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{k}\varphi_{\gamma}|\lesssim\frac{\delta^{l}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{t}}}\sum_{a\leq 1}\|R^{a}Z^{k}\varphi_{\gamma}\|_{L^{2}(S_{\mathfrak{t},u})}{\lesssim}\frac{\delta^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mathfrak{t}}}\big(\sqrt{E_{1,\leq 2N-4}}+\sqrt{E_{2,\leq 2N-4}}\big)\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},

(7.277)

which is independent of
M
.
This closes the bootstrap assumptions
(\star)
, and hence the uniform estimates and existence of the solution

\phi
to (
1.8
) with (
1.2
) and (
1.9
)-(
1.10
) in the domain
D^{{\mathfrak{t}},4\delta}
can be proved by
the standard continuity argument (see Figure
3
in Subsection
2.2
).

Finally, let
\Gamma\in\{(t+r)L,\underline{L},\Omega\}
be defined in the end of Section
1
. For any
(t,x)\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
, we will refine the estimates on
\mid\Gamma^{\alpha}\phi\mid
,
\Omega^{k}\phi
and
L\Omega^{k}\phi
with better smallness as

\begin{split}|\Gamma^{\alpha}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\quad|\alpha|\leq 2N-9,\\
|(\Omega^{k}\phi,tL\Omega^{k}\phi)(t,x)|\lesssim&\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\quad k\leq 2N-9,\end{split}

(7.278)

which will be crucial to derive the global estimates and existence of the solution
\phi
to
(
1.8
)
in
B_{2\delta}
.

First, we improve the estimates on derivatives of
\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}
,

\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}
and
u-(\mathfrak{t}-r)
.

Using (
7.276
) again, one can get by (
4.161
) that

\begin{split}&\|\varrho\delta^{l}Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{{\mathfrak{t}},u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l+1/2}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\Big(\|\mathring{\underline{L}}(\varrho R^{\leq 1}Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}))\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\|\mathring{L}(\varrho R^{\leq 1}Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}))\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\Big)\\
\lesssim&\delta^{l+1/2}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\Big\{\|R^{\leq 1}Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\varrho\|[\mathring{\underline{L}},R]Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\\
&\qquad\qquad\quad+\varrho\|R^{\leq 1}[\mathring{\underline{L}},Z^{\beta}](\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\varrho\|R^{\leq 1}Z^{\beta}\mathring{\underline{L}}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\Big\}.\end{split}

Since
\mathring{\underline{L}}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})=(\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{L}^{\alpha})\varphi_{\alpha}+\mathring{\underline{L}}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha}
and
\mathring{\underline{L}}\mathring{L}^{\alpha}
is a
combination of (
2.62
) and (
2.68
), for
|\beta|\leq 2N-7
,
one then has

\begin{split}&\|\varrho\delta^{l}Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})\|_{L^{\infty}(S_{{\mathfrak{t}},u})}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{1/2}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\big\{\delta^{l_{2}}\|Z^{n_{2}}\varphi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{1}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|Z^{n_{1}}\check{L}^{i}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\big\}\\
&+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{2}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\big\{\|Z^{n_{2}}\mu\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}+\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\|Z^{n_{2}}x\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}^{u})}\big\}\\
&+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}+l_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1}\big\{\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(\bar{Z})}\not{\pi}_{\mathring{L}}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}+\|\not{\mathcal{L}}_{Z}^{n_{0}}{\vphantom{\not{\pi}}}{}^{(R)}\not{\pi}_{T}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{u})}\big\}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}+\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{1,\leq 2N-4}}+\delta^{3/2}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}\sqrt{\tilde{E}_{2,\leq 2N-4}}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\end{split}

(7.279)

where
l_{i}
is the number of
T
in
Z^{n_{i}}
and
n_{i}\leq|\beta|+i

(i=0,1,2)
. (
7.279
) implies that
in the domain
D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}
,
\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}
can be estimated more precisely as

\delta^{l}|Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha})|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2},\quad|\beta|\leq 2N-7.

(7.280)

Similarly, it holds that

\delta^{l}|Z^{\beta}(\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\mathring{L}\varphi_{\alpha})|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-5/2},\quad|\beta|\leq 2N-8.

(7.281)

In addition, it follows from (
2.63
) and (
7.281
) that for
|\beta|\leq 2N-8
,

\delta^{l}|Z^{\beta}\check{L}^{a}|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1},

(7.282)

which leads to

\mid Z^{\beta}\big(1-\frac{r}{\varrho}\big)\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1},\quad|\beta|\leq 2N-8

(7.283)

by (
3.106
) and
\varphi_{0}+\varphi_{i}\omega^{i}=\mathring{L}^{\alpha}\varphi_{\alpha}-\varphi_{i}\check{L}^{i}+\varphi_{i}\omega^{i}(1-\frac{r}{\varrho})=O(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-3/2})+\varphi_{i}\omega^{i}(1-\frac{r}{\varrho})
.
It follows from (
7.283
) that the distance between
C_{0}
and
C_{4\delta}
on the hypersurface
\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}
is
4\delta+O(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}})
and the characteristic
surface
C_{u}

(0\leq u\leq 4\delta)
is almost straight with the error
O(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}})
from
the corresponding outgoing conic surface.

On the other hand, note that

\begin{split}&L=\mathring{L}+\mu^{-1}\big\{(1-\frac{r}{\varrho})-g_{ij}\check{L}^{i}\tilde{T}^{j}+(\frac{\varrho}{r}-1)g_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\tilde{T}^{j}\big\}T+\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\check{T}^{i}(\not{d}^{X}x^{j})X,\\
&\underline{L}=\mathring{L}+\mu^{-1}\big\{1+\frac{\varrho}{r}-c^{-1}\varphi_{i}\tilde{T}^{i}-\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\check{T}^{i}\tilde{T}^{j}\big\}T-\frac{\varrho}{r}g_{ij}\check{T}^{i}(\not{d}^{X}x^{j})X,\\
&\Omega=R-\mu^{-1}g_{ab}{\epsilon_{i}^{a}}x^{i}\check{L}^{b}T+\mu^{-1}c^{-1}\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a}T.\end{split}

(7.284)

When
|\beta|\leq 2N-7
,

\begin{split}TZ^{\beta}(\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})=&[T,Z^{\beta}](\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})+Z^{\beta}T(\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})\\
=&\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=|\beta|-1}Z^{\beta_{1}}[T,Z]Z^{\beta_{2}}(\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})+Z^{\beta}(\mu\epsilon_{i}^{a}\tilde{T}^{i}\varphi_{a})\\
&+Z^{\beta}\big(T^{\alpha}R\varphi_{\alpha}+g_{mn}\epsilon_{j}^{m}x^{j}\check{T}^{n}\tilde{T}^{a}T\varphi_{a}\big),\end{split}

and then
|TZ^{\beta}(\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}-l}
(
l
is the number of
T
in
Z^{\beta}
),
which implies that

|\underline{L}Z^{\beta}(\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})|\lesssim\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}-l},\quad|\beta|\leq 2N-7

(7.285)

due to the second equation in (
7.284
) and (
7.277
). Integrate (
7.285
)
along integral curves of
\underline{L}
, and use the
zero boundary value on
C_{0}
to get

|Z^{\beta}(\epsilon_{i}^{a}x^{i}\varphi_{a})|\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-l},\quad|\beta|\leq 2N-7.

(7.286)

Therefore, in
D^{{\mathfrak{t}},u}
, collecting (
7.284
), (
7.283
), (
7.282
), (
7.286
) and (
7.277
) yields

\mid\Gamma^{\alpha}\varphi_{\gamma}\mid\lesssim\delta^{1-l-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2},\quad|\alpha|\leq 2N-7,

(7.287)

where
l
is the number of
\underline{L}
in
\Gamma^{\alpha}
.

Recall that
\phi
is the solution of (
1.8
) and
\varphi_{\gamma}=\partial_{\gamma}\phi
. Thus (
7.287
)
implies that for
|\alpha|\leq 2N-7
,

\mid\underline{L}\Gamma^{\alpha}\phi\mid\lesssim\delta^{1-l-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}.

(7.288)

Hence, as for (
7.286
), one can get that in
D^{{\mathfrak{t}},4\delta}
, for
|\alpha|\leq 2N-7
,

\mid\Gamma^{\alpha}\phi\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-l-\varepsilon_{0}}\mathfrak{t}^{-1/2}.

(7.289)

For any point
P(t^{0},x^{0})\in\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,
there is an integral line of

L
across this point and the initial point is denoted by
P_{0}(t_{0},x_{0})

on
\Sigma_{t_{0}}
with
|x_{0}|=1
.
It follows from (
2.30
) that

\mid L(r^{1/2}{\partial^{\alpha}\underline{L}}\phi)\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-3/2},\quad|\alpha|\leq 1.

(7.290)

Integrating (
7.290
) along integral curves of
L
and applying (
2.23
) to show
that on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

\mid{\partial^{\alpha}\underline{L}}\phi\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-1/2},\quad|\alpha|\leq 1.

(7.291)

Using (
7.291
) and (
2.30
) again gives
\mid L(r^{1/2}{\underline{L}}\phi)\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
,
which implies in turn that

|\underline{L}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},

and hence,
\mid L{\underline{L}}\phi\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
holds by (
2.30
).
An induction argument and (
2.30
) show

\mid\Gamma^{\alpha}\phi\mid\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\quad|\alpha|\leq 2N-9,\ \text{on}\ {\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}.

In addition, it follows from

\begin{split}&\underline{L}(\frac{1}{r}\phi+2L\phi)=\frac{1}{r^{2}}\phi+\frac{1}{r}L\phi+\frac{2}{r^{2}}\Omega^{2}\phi+2(g^{\alpha\beta,\gamma}\partial_{\gamma}\phi+h^{\alpha\beta,\nu\gamma}\partial_{\nu}\phi\partial_{\gamma}\phi)\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\phi\end{split}

and (
7.287
) that on
D^{0}=\{(t,x):0\leq t-|x|\leq 2\delta,t+|x|\geq 2+2\delta\}
,

|\underline{L}(\frac{1}{r}\Omega^{k}\phi+2L\Omega^{k}\phi)|_{D^{0}}\lesssim\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-2},\ k\leq 2N-9.

(7.292)

Integrating (
7.292
) along integral curves of
\underline{L}
in
D^{0}
yields

|\frac{1}{r}\Omega^{k}\phi+2L\Omega^{k}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-2},\ k\leq 2N-9,

(7.293)

which means
|L(r^{1/2}\Omega^{k}\phi)|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
, and then

|\Omega^{k}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}\ \text{for}\ k\leq 2N-9

(7.294)

thanks to (
2.22
).
It follows from (
7.293
)-(
7.294
) directly that

|L\Omega^{k}\phi|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}\ \text{for}\ k\leq 2N-9.

Thus, (
7.278
) is proved.

8
Global estimates inside
B_{2\delta}
and the proof of Theorem
1.1

In this section, we derive the global estimates and existence of the solution
\phi
to (
1.8
) inside
B_{2\delta}
.
It is emphasized that different from the problem of 2D or 3D small value solutions inside the cone in
[
9
]

and
[
12
]
, here the solution
\phi
to (
1.8
) may be large in
B_{2\delta}

due to the short pulse initial data on time
t_{0}
. Nonetheless,
we still intend to make suitable energy assumptions and use Sobolev embedding to estimate
L^{\infty}
norm of the solution
in
B_{2\delta}
. To close the energy assumptions, we will control the power of
\delta
in
L^{\infty}
norm of the solution, and hence
a modified Klainerman-Sobolev Lemma is introduced whose proof is similar to those in
[
11
]
.
However, the energies used in
[
11
]
are not suitable for (
1.8
) due to the slow time decay of the related quantities in 2D.
Making crucial use of the two null conditions for (
1.8
), we can establish some classes of global weighted spacetime energy estimates here.

Define

D_{t}:=\{(\bar{t},x):\bar{t}-|x|\geq 2\delta,t_{0}\leq\bar{t}\leq t\}\subset B_{2\delta}

to be the shaded part in Figure
4
below. Note that for
\delta>0
small, the
L^{\infty}
norm of
\phi
and
its first order derivatives are small on the boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
of
B_{2\delta}
(especially,
\Gamma^{\alpha}\phi

admits the better smallness

O(\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}})
on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
, see (
7.278
)).

Figure 4:
The domain
D_{t}
inside
B_{2\delta}

As in
[
11
, Lemma 12.1]
(compared to
[
24
, Proposition 3.1]
,
the refined inner estimate is new due to the appearance of factor
\delta^{(i-1)s}
),
the following modified Klainerman-Sobolev inequalities hold true.

Lemma 8.1
.

For any function
f(t,x)\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{1+2})
,
t\geq 1
,
(t,x)\in D_{T}=\{(t,x):t-|x|\geq 2\delta,t_{0}\leq t\leq T\}
, the following inequalities hold:

\mid f(t,x)\mid\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{2}t^{-1}\delta^{(i-1)s}\|\bar{\Gamma}^{i}f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(r\leq t/2)},\ |x|\leq\frac{1}{4}t,

(8.295)

\mid f(t,x)\mid\lesssim\mid f(t,B_{t}^{x})\mid+\sum_{a\leq 1,|\beta|\leq 1}t^{-1/2}\|\Omega^{a}\partial^{\beta}f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)},\ |x|\geq\frac{1}{4}t,

(8.296)

where
\bar{\Gamma}\in\{S,H_{1},H_{2},\Omega\}
,
(t,B_{t}^{x})
is the intersection point of
the boundary
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
and the ray crossing
(t,x)
which emanates from
(t,0)
,
and
s
is the any nonnegative constant in (
8.295
).

We also need the following inequality which is similar to Lemma 2.3 for 3D case in
[
10
]
.

Lemma 8.2
.

For
f(t,x)\in C^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{1+2})
and
t\geq 1
, it holds that for
1\leq\bar{t}\leq t-2\delta
,

\|\frac{f(t,\cdot)}{1+t-|\cdot|}\|_{L^{2}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}\lesssim t^{1/2}\|f(t,B_{t}^{\cdot})\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}+\|\partial f(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\bar{t}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta)}.

(8.297)

We now apply the energy method to derive the global estimates and the existence of solution
\phi
to (
1.8
) in
B_{2\delta}
.
To this end, motivated by the works on global solutions with small data to 3D nonlinear wave equations
satisfying the first null condition in
[
20
,
7
]
, one can define the energy as

E_{k,l}(t)=\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,\cdot)\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}^{2}+\iint_{D_{t}}\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|^{2}(t^{\prime},x)}{1+(t^{\prime}-|x|)^{3/2}}dxdt^{\prime},

(8.298)

where
\tilde{Z}\in\{\tilde{Z}_{i}=\omega^{i}\partial_{t}+\partial_{i},i=1,2\}
,
\tilde{\Gamma}\in\{\partial,S,H_{1},H_{2}\}
. Based on the
estimate (
2.22
) on
\Sigma_{t_{0}}
, one can assume that for
t\geq t_{0}
, there exists a uniform constant
M_{0}
such that

\begin{split}E_{k,l}(t)\leq{M_{0}}^{2}\delta^{2a_{k}},\quad k+l\leq 5\end{split}

(8.299)

with
a_{k}=\frac{5}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}-k
.

According to Lemma
8.1
,
8.2
, and (
8.299
), we can obtain
the following
L^{\infty}
estimates.

Proposition 8.1
.

Under the assumptions (
8.299
), when
\delta>0
is small, it holds that in
B_{2\delta}
,

\begin{split}&|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\quad k+l\leq 3\end{split}

(8.300)

and

\begin{split}&|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1/2},\quad k+l\leq 3.\end{split}

(8.301)

Proof.

First, for
|x|\leq\frac{t}{4}
, one gets from (
8.295
) that

\begin{split}&|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\\
\lesssim&t^{-1}\Big\{\delta^{-s}\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}+\|\bar{\Gamma}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}+\delta^{s}\|\bar{\Gamma}^{2}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}\big\}.\end{split}

(8.302)

Choosing
s=1
in (
8.302
) and utilizing (
8.299
) yield

|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1},\ k+l\leq 3.

(8.303)

Next, for
\frac{t}{4}\leq|x|\leq t-2\delta
, choosing
f(t,x)=(1+t-|x|)^{-1}|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,x)|
in (
8.296
),
and applying (
8.297
) to
\Omega^{\leq 1}\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,x)
, one can get

\begin{split}&\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,x)|}{1+t-|x|}\lesssim|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,B_{t}^{x})|+t^{-1/2}\|\frac{\Omega^{\leq 1}\partial^{\leq 1}\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi}{1+t-r}\|_{L^{2}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)}\\
\lesssim&\|\Omega^{\leq 1}\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,B_{t}^{\cdot})\|_{L^{\infty}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)}+t^{-1/2}\|\partial\Omega^{\leq 1}\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi\|_{L^{2}(t/4\leq r\leq t-2\delta)}.\end{split}

(8.304)

Since
\omega^{i}\partial_{t}+\partial_{i}=\omega^{i}L+\frac{1}{r}\omega^{i}_{\perp}\Omega
,
|\Omega^{\leq 1}\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,B_{t}^{x})|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}
due to (
7.278
), then it follows from (
8.304
) and (
8.299
) that

\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,x)|}{1+t-|x|}\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2},\ k+l\leq 3.

(8.305)

In addition, (
8.296
) implies directly that

|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1/2},\ k+l\leq 3.

(8.306)

Thus it follows from (
8.303
), (
8.305
) and (
8.306
) that

\displaystyle|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\ k+l\leq 3,

\displaystyle|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1/2},\ k+l\leq 3.

∎

Corollary 8.1
.

Under the same conditions in Proposition
8.1
, it holds that in
B_{2\delta}
,

|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1/2}(1+t-r)^{-1},\ k+l\leq 2

(8.307)

and

|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2},\ k+l\leq 2.

(8.308)

Proof.

These results follow from

\partial_{i}=\frac{1}{1+t-r}\partial_{i}-\frac{1}{1+t-r}\big(\frac{x^{i}}{t+r}S-\frac{t}{t+r}H_{i}+\frac{x_{\perp}^{i}}{t+r}\Omega\big)\quad\text{with}\ x_{\perp}=(-x^{2},x^{1})

(8.309)

and (
8.300
)-(
8.301
) directly.
∎

One can now carry out the energy estimates in
D_{t}
by using the ghost weight in
[
2
]
.
Indeed, choosing a multiplier
W\partial_{t}v
with
W=e^{2(1+t-r)^{-1/2}}
and integrating

W\partial_{t}vg^{\alpha\beta}(\partial\phi)\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v
over
D_{t}
yield

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}\frac{1}{2}\Big\{(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+(1+2\partial_{t}\phi+|\nabla\phi|^{2})|\nabla v|^{2}-\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\phi\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v\Big\}W\\
&+\iint_{D_{t}}\frac{1}{2}W\sum_{i}\frac{|\tilde{Z}_{i}v|^{2}}{(1+\tau-r)^{3/2}}\\
=&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}}\frac{1}{2}\Big\{(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+(1+2\partial_{t}\phi+|\nabla\phi|^{2})|\nabla v|^{2}-\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\phi\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{i}v\partial_{j}v\Big\}W\\
&+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\Big\{\frac{1}{2}(1+2\partial_{t}\phi+|\nabla\phi|^{2})\big((Lv)^{2}+\frac{(\Omega v)^{2}}{r^{2}}\big)-\big(L\phi+\frac{(\Omega\phi)^{2}}{2r^{2}}\big)(\partial_{t}v)^{2}\\
&\qquad-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\phi\partial_{j}\phi\tilde{Z}_{i}v\tilde{Z}_{j}v\Big\}W\\
&+\iint_{D_{t}}\frac{1}{2}\Big\{\sum_{i,j}(-2\partial_{t}\phi\delta^{ij}+\partial_{i}\phi\partial_{j}\phi-|\nabla\phi|^{2}\delta^{ij})\tilde{Z}_{i}v\tilde{Z}_{j}v-(\omega^{i}\omega^{j}\\
&\qquad\qquad-\delta^{ij})\tilde{Z}_{i}\phi\tilde{Z}_{j}\phi(\partial_{t}v)^{2}+2\omega^{i}(\tilde{Z}_{i}\phi)(\partial_{t}v)^{2}\Big\}\frac{W}{(1+\tau-r)^{3/2}}\\
&-\iint_{D_{t}}W\partial_{t}vg^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v+\iint_{D_{t}}\Big\{O(\tilde{Z}\partial\phi\cdot\partial v\cdot\partial v)+O(\partial\phi\cdot\tilde{Z}\partial\phi\cdot\partial v\cdot\partial v)\Big\}W\\
&+\iint_{D_{t}}\partial_{t}^{2}\phi\Big\{(1-\omega^{k}\partial_{k}\phi)\sum_{i}(\tilde{Z}_{i}v)^{2}+\sum_{i,j}\omega^{i}\partial_{j}\phi\tilde{Z}_{i}v\tilde{Z}_{j}v\Big\}W.\end{split}

(8.310)

Note that due to (
8.301
), the integrand of
\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}
