in (
8.310
) is equivalent to

\big((\partial_{t}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)W,

(8.311)

while the integrand of
\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}
in (
8.310
) can be controlled by

\big\{(Lv)^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}(\Omega v)^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}\big\}W

(8.312)

with the help of (
7.278
) and
\tilde{Z}_{i}=\omega^{i}L+\frac{\omega^{i}_{\perp}}{r}\Omega
, where the constant coefficients have been neglected.

Inserting (
8.311
) and (
8.312
) into (
8.310
), and using
Proposition
8.1
and Corollary
8.1
,
one then can get by Gronwall’s inequality that for small
\delta>0
,

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}\big((\partial_{t}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)W+\iint_{D_{t}}\sum_{i}\frac{|\tilde{Z}_{i}v|^{2}}{(1+\tau-r)^{3/2}}W\\
\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}}\big((\partial_{t}v)^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}\big)W+\iint_{D_{t}}\mid W\partial_{t}vg^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}v\mid\\
&+\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\big\{(Lv)^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}(\Omega v)^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}\big\}W.\end{split}

(8.313)

To close the bootstrap assumptions (
8.299
), one will apply (
8.313
) to
v=\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi

(k+l\leq 6)
.
By (
7.278
),

|(L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}(\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)^{2}|\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} \delta^{6-4\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-5/2},\ k=0,\\
\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-5/2},\ k\geq 1\end{aligned}\right.

hold on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
. Therefore,

\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\big\{(Lv)^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}(\Omega v)^{2}+\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}\tau^{-3/2}(\partial_{t}v)^{2}\big\}W\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} \delta^{6-4\varepsilon_{0}},&\ k=0,\ l\leq 6,\\
\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}},&\ k\geq 1,\ k+l\leq 6.\end{aligned}\right.

(8.314)

In addition, on the initial hypersurface
\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}
,
it holds that
|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{2-k-\varepsilon_{0}}

for
k+l\leq 6
by (
2.22
). Hence, (
8.313
) gives that for
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
,

\displaystyle E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k}+\iint_{D_{t}}\mid(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)\mid,\quad k+l\leq 6.

(8.315)

It remains to estimate
\iint_{D_{t}}\mid(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)\mid

in (
8.315
).

Theorem 8.1
.

Under the assumptions (
8.299
) with
\delta>0
small, it holds that

E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{2a_{k}}t^{2\iota},\quad k+l\leq 6,

(8.316)

where
a_{k}

(k=0,1,\cdots,5)
are those constants defined in (
8.299
),
a_{6}=-\frac{7}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}
, and
\iota

is some constant multiple of
\delta^{\varsigma}
with
0<\varsigma<1-2\varepsilon_{0}
.

Proof.

Acting the operator
\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}
on (
1.8
) and commuting it with
g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}
yield

\begin{split}g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi=&\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k,l_{1}+l_{2}\leq l\\
k_{2}+l_{2}<k+l\end{array}$}}G_{1}(\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi,\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi)\\
&+\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}\leq k\\
l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}\leq l\\
k_{3}+l_{3}<k+l\end{array}$}}G_{2}(\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi,\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi,\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{3}}\Omega^{l_{3}}\phi),\end{split}

where
G_{1}
is a generic quadratic form satisfying the first null condition, and
G_{2}
is a generic cubic form
satisfying the second null condition. Hence it follows from Lemma 2.2 in
[
17
]
that

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}\mid(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)(g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha\beta}^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi)\mid\\
\lesssim&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\Big\{\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k,l_{1}+l_{2}\leq l\\
k_{2}+l_{2}<k+l\end{array}$}}(|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|)\\
&+\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}\leq k\\
l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}\leq l\\
k_{3}+l_{3}<k+l\end{array}$}}(|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{3}}\Omega^{l_{3}}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{3}}\Omega^{l_{3}}\phi|)\Big\}.\end{split}

(8.317)

To estimate the right hand side of (
8.317
), one can check easily that in (
8.317
), the
last summation has better smallness and time decay, so one has only to deal with
the first summation.

If
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq k_{2}+l_{2}
, then
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq 3
. Thus Proposition
8.1
implies that

|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k_{1}}t^{-3/2}(1+t-r),\quad|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k_{1}}t^{-1/2}.

Then, (
8.309
) and (
8.299
) imply that since
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
and
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 5
, thus

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\big\{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\big\}\\
\lesssim&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k_{1}}M_{0}\big\{\tau^{-3/2}|(1+\tau-r)\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|+\tau^{-1/2}|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\big\}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{a=0}^{6-k}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{k,a}(\tau)d\tau+\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k}\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k_{1}}M_{0}^{2}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2(k_{2}+1)}M_{0}^{2}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k}+\sum_{a=0}^{6-k}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{k,a}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(8.318)

If
k_{1}+l_{1}>k_{2}+l_{2}
, then
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 2
. It follows from Corollary
8.1
that

|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k_{2}}t^{-1/2}(1+t-r)^{-1},\quad|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\lesssim M_{0}\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k_{2}}t^{-3/2},

which imply that there exists a
\varsigma\in(0,1-2\varepsilon_{0})
such that

\begin{split}&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\big\{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\big\}\\
\lesssim&\iint_{D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k_{2}}\tau^{-1/2}M_{0}\big\{\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|}{1+\tau-r}+\tau^{-1}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\big\}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{\varsigma}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-1}E_{k,l}(\tau)d\tau+\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k}M_{0}^{2}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k_{2}-\varsigma}E_{k_{1},l_{1}}(t)+\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{k,l}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k}M_{0}^{2}\delta^{1-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k_{2}}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{k_{1},l_{1}}(\tau)d\tau\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k}+\sum_{a=0}^{6-k}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-3/2}E_{k,a}(\tau)d\tau+\delta^{\varsigma}\int_{t_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-1}E_{k,l}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(8.319)

Substituting (
8.318
) and (
8.319
) into (
8.315
) yields

\begin{split}&\sum_{l=0}^{6-k}E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k}t^{2\iota},\end{split}

(8.320)

which proves (
8.316
).

∎

To prove the global estimates of the solution in
B_{2\delta}
, one needs to get
E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{2a_{k}},\ k+l\leq 5
which are independent of
M_{0}
for all time. Since (
8.316
) has been proved, then it holds that

E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{2a_{k}},\ k+l\leq 5\ \text{and}\ t_{0}\leq t\leq e^{1/\iota}.

(8.321)

It remains to show that the inequalities in (
8.321
) for
t\geq e^{1/\iota}
.

As for the
M_{0}
-independent energy estimate in (
8.316
), one can also obtain the
M_{0}
independent
L^{\infty}
estimates
of
\phi
and its derivatives corresponding to Proposition
8.1
and Corollary
8.1
.

Corollary 8.2
.

When
\delta>0
is small, it holds that in the domain
B_{2\delta}
,

|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2+\iota}(1+t-r),\ |\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1/2+\iota}\quad\text{for}\ k+l\leq 3\\

(8.322)

and

\begin{split}|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-1/2+\iota}(1+t-r)^{-1},\ |\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1/2-\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2+\iota},\quad\text{for}\ k+l\leq 2.\end{split}

(8.323)

Compared with (
8.299
), Proposition
8.1
and Corollary
8.1
, though the estimates in (
8.316
)
and Corollary
8.2
do not depend on
M_{0}
, yet they contain increasing time factors
t^{2\iota}
or
t^{\iota}
.
To overcome this difficulty and close the assumptions (
8.299
), we now study the equation on the difference
between
\phi
and
\phi_{a}
, where
\phi_{a}
satisfies

\left\{\begin{aligned} &-\partial_{t}^{2}\phi_{a}+\triangle\phi_{a}=0,\\
&\phi_{a}(1,x)=\phi(1,x),\\
&\partial_{t}\phi_{a}(1,x)=\partial_{t}\phi(1,x).\end{aligned}\right.

(8.324)

Proposition 8.2
.

\phi_{a}
defined by (
8.324
) satisfies the following estimate on the hypersurface
\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}
:

\begin{split}\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}dx&+\iint_{D_{t}}\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}(\tau,x)|^{2}}{(1+\tau-|x|)^{3/2}}dxd\tau\\
&+\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\big(|L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}\big)\lesssim\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2k}.\end{split}

(8.325)

Proof.

Exactly similar to Theorem
2.1
, (
8.324
) has a local smooth solution
\phi_{a}
on
[1,t_{0}]
satisfying

\displaystyle|L^{s}\partial^{q}\Omega^{k}\phi_{a}(t_{0},x)|\lesssim\delta^{2-|q|-\varepsilon_{0}},\quad|x|\in[1-2\delta,1+2\delta],

(8.326)

\displaystyle|\partial^{q}\Omega^{k}\phi_{a}(t_{0},x)|\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|q|},\quad|x|\in[1-3\delta,1+\delta].

(8.327)

Recall that

D^{0}=\{(t,x)|0\leq t-|x|\leq 2\delta,t+|x|\geq 2+2\delta\}.

When
t\geq t_{0}
, one can estimate
\phi_{a}(t,x)
in
D^{0}
by the standard energy method. Indeed, it holds that

\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D^{0}}|\partial\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D^{0}}\big(|L\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2}\big)=\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D^{0}}|\partial\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2},

(8.328)

where
\hat{Z}\in\{\partial,\Omega,S,H_{1},H_{2}\}
. If follows from (
8.326
) that
|\partial\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D^{0}}\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}-l}
with
l
being the number of
\partial
in
\hat{Z}^{\alpha}
. Therefore, (
8.328
) implies

\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D^{0}}|\partial\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D^{0}}\big(|L\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|^{2}\big)\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}-2l}.

(8.329)

By the following Sobolev’s imbedding theorem on the circle
\mathbb{S}^{1}_{r}

(with center at the origin and radius
r
)

|w(t,x)|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{r}}\|\Omega^{\leq 1}w\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{S}^{1}_{r})},

one can get that for any point
(t,x)
in
D^{0}
,

|\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}(t,x)|\lesssim t^{-1/2}\|\Omega^{\leq 1}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{S}_{r})}\lesssim t^{-1/2}\delta^{1/2}\|\partial\Omega^{\leq 1}\hat{Z}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\mathfrak{t}}\cap D^{0})}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-l}t^{-1/2}.

(8.330)

In addition,
\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}
solves

L\big(r^{1/2}\underline{L}\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}\big)=\frac{1}{2}r^{-1/2}L\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\phi_{a},

which implies
|L\big(r^{1/2}\underline{L}\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}\big)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-2}
by (
8.330
) since
\bar{\Gamma}\in\{S,H_{1},H_{2},\Omega\}
. And hence, on the surface
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
,

|\underline{L}\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}
holds
after integrating
L\big(r^{1/2}\underline{L}\bar{\Gamma}^{\alpha}\phi_{a}\big)
along integrate curves of
L
on
\tilde{C}_{2\delta}
. Then,

|L\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|+\frac{1}{r}|\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}\quad\text{on}\ \tilde{C}_{2\delta}.

By an induction argument, one can get

|L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|+\frac{1}{r}|\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}\quad\text{on}\ \tilde{C}_{2\delta}.

Following the proof for (
7.292
)-(
7.294
), one has

|\Omega^{k}\phi_{a}|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\ |L\Omega^{k}\phi_{a}|_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-3/2}.

Therefore,

\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}\big(|L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}\big)\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} \delta^{6-2\varepsilon_{0}},&\ k=0,\\
\delta^{4-2\varepsilon_{0}},&\ k\geq 1.\end{aligned}\right.

(8.331)

It follows from the property of the weight function
W
in (
8.310
) and (
8.324
) that

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t}}W|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\iint_{D_{t}}W\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}}{(1+\tau-r)^{3/2}}\\
=&\int_{\Sigma_{t_{0}}\cap D_{t}}W|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap D_{t}}W\big(|L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\phi_{a}|^{2}\big).\end{split}

(8.332)

Then the estimate (
8.325
) is a direct consequence of (
8.327
) and (
8.331
)-(
8.332
).
∎

Similarly as for Proposition
8.1
, one can use Lemma
8.1
,
8.2
and Proposition
8.2
to
get the
L^{\infty}
estimate of

\phi-\phi_{a}
in

\tilde{D}_{t}:=\{(\bar{t},x):\bar{t}-|x|\geq 2\delta,T_{0}\leq\bar{t}\leq t\}

with
t\geq T_{0}
and
T_{0}=e^{1/\iota}
.
Indeed, let
\dot{\phi}=\phi-\phi_{a}
. Then
\dot{\phi}
solves

\left\{\begin{aligned} &-\partial_{t}^{2}\dot{\phi}+\triangle\dot{\phi}=2\sum_{i=1}^{2}\partial_{i}\phi\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\phi-2\partial_{t}\phi\triangle\phi+\sum_{i,j=1}^{2}\partial_{i}\phi\partial_{j}\phi\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\phi-|\nabla\phi|^{2}\triangle\phi,\\
&\dot{\phi}(T_{0},x)=\partial_{t}\dot{\phi}(T_{0},x)=0.\end{aligned}\right.

(8.333)

Proposition 8.3
.

If
\delta>0
is small and
k+l\leq 5
, then for
t\geq T_{0}
,

\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap\tilde{D}_{t})}^{2}+\iint_{\tilde{D}_{t}}\frac{|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|^{2}(t^{\prime},x)}{1+(t^{\prime}-|x|)^{3/2}}dxdt^{\prime}\lesssim\delta^{2a_{k}}.

(8.334)

Proof.

By commuting the operator
\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}
with
-\partial_{t}^{2}+\triangle
,
and noting that the right hand side of (
8.333
) satisfies the first and second null conditions,
one gets
from (
8.322
) and (
8.323
) that

\begin{split}&|(-\partial_{t}^{2}+\triangle)\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|\\
\lesssim&\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k\\
l_{1}+l_{2}\leq l\end{array}$}}\big(|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\big)\\
&+\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}+k_{3}\leq k\\
l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}\leq l\end{array}$}}\big(|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdot|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{3}}\Omega^{l_{3}}\phi|\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\cdot|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\cdot|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{3}}\Omega^{l_{3}}\phi|\big)\\
\lesssim&\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k\\
l_{1}+l_{2}\leq l\\
k_{1}+l_{1}\leq 2\end{array}$}}\big\{\delta^{\varpi_{k_{1}}}t^{-3/2+\iota}(1+t-r)|\partial^{2}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|+\delta^{\varpi_{k_{1}}}t^{-1/2+\iota}|\tilde{Z}\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{2}}\Omega^{l_{2}}\phi|\big\}\\
&+\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}k_{1}+k_{2}\leq k\\
l_{1}+l_{2}\leq l\\
k_{2}+l_{2}\leq 2\end{array}$}}\big\{\delta^{\varpi_{k_{2}+1}}t^{-1/2+\iota}(1+t-r)^{-1}|\tilde{Z}\Gamma^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|+\delta^{\varpi_{k_{2}+1}}t^{-3/2+\iota}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k_{1}}\Omega^{l_{1}}\phi|\big\},\end{split}

(8.335)

where
\varpi_{k}=\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}-k
,
k=0,1,2,3
. And hence,

\begin{split}&\|(-\partial_{t}^{2}+\triangle)\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}\\
\lesssim&\sum_{s=0}^{\min\{2,k\}}\delta^{\varpi_{s}}t^{-3/2+\iota}\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}p+q\leq 6\\
p\leq k-s+1\end{array}$}}\sqrt{E_{p,q}(t)}\\
&+\sum_{s=1}^{\min\{k+1,3\}}\delta^{\varpi_{s}}t^{-1/2+\iota}\sum_{\mbox{\tiny$\begin{array}[]{cc}p+q\leq 5\\
p\leq k-s+1\end{array}$}}\Big\{\|\frac{\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{q}\phi}{1+t-r}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap D_{t})}+t^{-1}\sqrt{E_{p,q}(t)}\Big\}.\end{split}

(8.336)

On the other hand, Lemma
8.2
and Theorem
8.1
imply that

\|\frac{\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{p}\Omega^{q}\phi}{1+t-r}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap\tilde{D}_{t})}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1}+\delta^{a_{p+1}}t^{-1+\iota}.

(8.337)

Substituting (
8.337
) and (
8.316
) into (
8.336
) yields

\begin{split}\|(-\partial_{t}^{2}+\triangle)\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{t}\cap\tilde{D}_{t})}\lesssim&\sum_{s=0}^{\min\{2,k\}}\delta^{\varpi_{s}}\delta^{a_{k-s+1}}t^{-3/2+2\iota}+\sum_{s=1}^{\min\{k+1,3\}}\delta^{\varpi_{s}}\delta^{a_{k-s+2}}t^{-3/2+2\iota}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{2-2\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-3/2+2\iota}\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}-k}t^{-7/6+2\iota}\end{split}

(8.338)

since
t\geq\delta^{-3}
for
t\geq T_{0}
.
Integrate
W(\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi})\big((-\partial_{t}^{2}+\triangle)\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}\big)
over
domain
\tilde{D}_{t}
with
W=e^{2(1+t-r)^{-1/2}}
as in (
8.310
). Then it follows from (
8.314
), (
8.325
) and (
8.338
) that
for
\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{2}
,

\begin{split}&\int_{\Sigma_{t}\cap\tilde{D}_{t}}W|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|^{2}+\iint_{\tilde{D}_{t}}\frac{W}{(1+\tau-r)^{3/2}}|\tilde{Z}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|^{2}\\
\lesssim&\int_{\Sigma_{T_{0}}\cap\tilde{D}_{t}}|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|^{2}+\iint_{\tilde{D}_{t}}W|\partial_{t}\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|\cdot|(-\partial_{t}^{2}+\triangle)\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|\end{split}

\begin{split}&+\int_{\tilde{C}_{2\delta}\cap\tilde{D}_{t}}\big(|L\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}|^{2}\big)\\
\lesssim&\int_{T_{0}}^{t}\tau^{-7/6+2\iota}\|\partial\tilde{\Gamma}^{k}\Omega^{l}\dot{\phi}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{\tau}\cap\tilde{D}_{t})}^{2}d\tau+\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}+2k}.\end{split}

(8.339)

Therefore, (
8.334
) follows from (
8.339
) directly since
\iota>0
is small enough.
∎

Theorem 8.2
.

When
\delta>0
is small, there exists a smooth solution
\phi
to (
1.8
) in
B_{2\delta}
for
t\geq t_{0}
.

Proof.

Since
\phi=\phi_{a}+\dot{\phi}
, then (
8.321
), Proposition
8.2
and
8.3
imply that

E_{k,l}(t)\lesssim\delta^{2a_{k}}\quad\text{for}\ k+l\leq 5,

(8.340)

which is independent of
M_{0}
, then the assumptions (
8.299
) are then improved.
∎

Finally, we prove Theorem
1.1
.

Proof.

Theorem
2.1
gives the local existence of smooth solution
\phi
to (
1.8
) with (
1.2
)
and (
1.9
)-(
1.10
).
On the other hand, the a priori global uniform estimates of the solution in
A_{2\delta}

and in
B_{2\delta}
have been established in Section
7
and Theorem
8.2
respectively.
Then it follows from the existence of the smooth solution to (
1.8
) and the continuous induction
argument that the proof of
\phi\in C^{\infty}([1,+\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{2})
is finished.
In addition,
|\nabla\phi|\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}
follows from (
2.20
), (
2.21
),
(
7.277
), and the first inequality in (
8.301
). Furthermore,
|\phi|\lesssim\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{1/2}
follows from (
7.289
), (
7.278
), (
8.301
) and the Newton-Leibnitz formula. Thus Theorem
1.1

is proved.

∎

Appendix A
Derivative estimate for the 2D wave equation with short pulse data

In this Appendix, we derive the derivative estimate for the solution to the 2D homogeneous wave equation
\square\phi=0

with the short pulse data (
1.2
) under the condition (
1.9
). Set

\displaystyle{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}:=\{(t,x):t\geq 1,x\in\mathbb{R}^{2}\},\quad{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}^{u}:=\{(t,x):0\leq\frac{t-r}{2}\leq u\},\ u\in[0,2\delta],

\displaystyle{\tilde{C}}_{u}^{t}:=\{(t^{\prime},x):1+2\delta\leq t^{\prime}\leq t,\frac{t-r}{2}=u\},\quad{\tilde{S}}_{t,u}:={\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}\cap{\tilde{C}}_{u}^{t},

\displaystyle I:=\{(t,x):1\leq t\leq 1+2\delta,2-\delta-t\leq r\leq t\},\quad II:=\{(t,x):t\geq 1+2\delta,t-4\delta\leq r\leq t\},

\displaystyle III:=\{(t,x):t\geq 1+2\delta,r\leq t-4\delta\},\quad IV=\{(t,x):1\leq t\leq 1+2\delta,r\leq 2-\delta-t\}.

Figure 5:
The Domains I, II, III, IV

Let
\phi
be the global smooth solution of
\square\phi=0
with initial data (
1.2
). Then it holds

\int_{{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}}|\partial\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}Z^{\beta}\phi(t,x)|^{2}dx=\int_{{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{1}}|\partial\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}Z^{\beta}\phi(1,x)|^{2}dx\lesssim\delta^{3-2\varepsilon_{0}-2|\alpha|},\ |\beta|\leq 1,

(A.2)

where
\Gamma\in\{\partial,S,H_{1},H_{2}\}
,
Z\in\{S,H_{1},H_{2}\}
, and condition (
1.9
) has been used.

{\bf\bullet}

In domain
I
, it follows from the embedding theorem on
\mathbb{S}^{1}
and (
A.2
) that when
|\beta|\leq 1
,

|\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}Z^{\beta}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\|\Omega^{\leq 1}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}Z^{\beta}\phi\|_{L^{2}({\tilde{S}}_{t,r})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\partial\Omega^{\leq 1}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}Z^{\beta}\phi\|_{L^{2}({\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t})}\lesssim\delta^{2-|\alpha|-\varepsilon_{0}}.

(A.3)

This implies that for
a=0,1
, due to
({t+r})L={S+\omega^{i}H_{i}}
, it holds that

|L^{a}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\sum_{|\beta|\leq 1}|Z^{\beta}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}.

(A.4)

In addition, in the domain
\tilde{D}=\{(t,x):1\leq t\leq 1+2\delta,2-\delta-t\leq r\leq t-\delta\}
, for
(t,x)\in\tilde{D}
, by

L(r^{1/2}\underline{L}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi)=\frac{1}{2}r^{-1/2}L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi+r^{-3/2}\Omega^{2}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi,

together with (
A.4
), one has
|L(r^{1/2}\underline{L}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi)|_{\tilde{D}}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}
. It follows from this and integration along integral curves of
L
that

|\underline{L}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|_{\tilde{D}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}.

(A.5)

Collecting (
A.4
) and (
A.5
) yields
|\partial\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|_{\tilde{D}}\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}
and

\|\partial\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi\|_{L^{2}({\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}\cap{\tilde{D}})}\lesssim\delta^{5/2-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}.

(A.6)

Similarly to (
A.5
), for
a=1
, integrating (
A.4
) along integral curves of
L
yields

|\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|_{\tilde{D}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}.

(A.7)

{\bf\bullet}
In domain
II
, similar to (
A.4
), one can obtain

\begin{split}&|\partial\phi(t,x)|\lesssim t^{-1/2}\|\partial\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi\|_{L^{2}({\tilde{S}}_{t,r})}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}t^{-1/2}\|\partial^{2}\Omega^{\leq 1}\phi\|_{L^{2}({\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}^{2\delta})}\lesssim\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2},\\
&|L^{a}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi(t,x)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-1/2-a},\ a=0,1.\end{split}

(A.8)

On the other hand, it follows from

\begin{split}&\underline{L}(\frac{1}{r}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi+2L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi)=\frac{1}{r^{2}}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi+\frac{1}{r}L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi+\frac{2}{r^{2}}\Omega^{2}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi\end{split}

and (
A.8
) that

|\underline{L}(\frac{1}{r}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi+2L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi)|\lesssim\delta^{2-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-5/2}.

This implies

|\frac{1}{r}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi+2L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-5/2}

(A.9)

and

|L(r^{1/2}\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi)|_{{\tilde{C}}_{2\delta}^{t}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-2}.

Therefore, thanks to (
A.7
), one has

|\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|_{{\tilde{C}}_{2\delta}^{t}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-1/2}.

(A.10)

Combining (
A.9
) with (
A.10
) shows that

|L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|_{{\tilde{C}}_{2\delta}^{t}}\lesssim\delta^{3-\varepsilon_{0}-|\alpha|}t^{-3/2}.

(A.11)

{\bf\bullet}
In domain
III
, one has that

\begin{split}&\int_{{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{t}\cap III}|\partial\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi(t,x)|^{2}dx\\
=&\int_{{\tilde{\Sigma}}_{1+2\delta}\cap{\tilde{D}}}|\partial\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi(1+2\delta,x)|^{2}dx+\int_{{\tilde{C}}_{2\delta}^{t}}\big(|L\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|^{2}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\Omega\Gamma^{\alpha}\Omega^{c}\phi|^{2}\big)dS\\
\lesssim&\delta^{5-2\varepsilon_{0}-2|\alpha|},\end{split}

(A.12)

where (
A.6
), (
A.10
) and (
A.11
) are used.

When
|x|\geq\frac{1}{4}t
, it follows from (
8.296
) and (
A.12
) that

|\partial\phi(t,x)|\leq\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}.

(A.13)

When
|x|\leq\frac{1}{4}t
, choose
s=1
in (
8.295
) and use (
A.12
) to obtain

|\partial\phi(t,x)|\leq\delta^{3/2-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1}.

(A.14)

{\bf\bullet}
In domain
IV
, by the finite propagation speed
of the wave equation
and the compact support of
(\phi_{0},\phi_{1})
, then for
(t,x)\in IV
, one has

\phi(t,x)\equiv 0.

(A.15)

Therefore, for any
(t,x)\in[1,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, by (
A.3
), (
A.8
), (
A.13
)-(
A.14
) and (
A.15
)
we get

|\partial\phi(t,x)|\leq\delta^{1-\varepsilon_{0}}t^{-1/2}.
